TL;DR: 本文利用IBM Heron r3超导量子处理器结合QESEM误差缓解软件，在多达74个量子比特的Floquet Ising磁体中测量了亚谐波预热振荡，精度达到百分级别，并发现振荡幅度随系统尺寸减小的速度异常缓慢，为热力学极限下持续振荡的存在提供了有力证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性驱动的量子多体系统中预热动力学的长寿命相干结构难以在大系统尺寸和长时间尺度下研究：经典张量网络方法无法收敛，稀疏Pauli路径模拟存在截断依赖，而量子硬件噪声会退化可观测量期望值。

Method: 在重六角晶格上实现Floquet Ising磁体，使用IBM Heron r3超导量子处理器结合QESEM误差缓解软件进行测量，采用多层级误差缓解与验证测试（包括无偏误差缓解、独立缓解估计量一致性、噪声模型验证以及Quantinuum离子阱硬件交叉验证）。

Result: 在多达74个量子比特系统中以百分级精度测量磁化动力学，分辨出长寿命亚谐波预热振荡；领先的张量网络模拟无法收敛，稀疏Pauli路径模拟在高性能计算资源下仍存在强截断依赖；有限尺寸标度发现振荡幅度随系统尺寸下降异常缓慢。

Conclusion: 误差缓解的量子处理器可作为定量科学仪器，用于发现非平衡量子物质中的新物理。

Abstract: Periodically driven interacting quantum many-body systems can exhibit long-lived prethermal dynamics, where local observables retain coherent structure even as entanglement and operator complexity grow. Accessing this regime at the system sizes and times needed to determine physical properties of the prethermal state remains a central challenge: state-of-the-art classical methods become unreliable, while noise in quantum hardware degrades observable expectation values. Here we overcome these limitations for a Floquet Ising magnet realized on a heavy-hex lattice. Using the advanced error mitigation software QESEM on an IBM Heron r3 superconducting quantum processor, we measure magnetization dynamics with percent-level precision and resolve long-lived subharmonic prethermal oscillations in systems of up to 74 qubits. These experiments reach regimes for which leading tensor-network simulations fail to converge, while sparse Pauli-path simulations remain strongly truncation dependent despite extensive computations on advanced GPUs and the Fugaku supercomputer. Leveraging this quantum-accessible regime, we extend finite-size scaling to larger systems and find an unexpectedly slow decrease of the oscillation amplitude with system size, providing strong evidence that this oscillatory response persists in the thermodynamic limit of heavy-hex ladders. A hierarchy of mitigation and validation tests, including unbiased error mitigation, agreement between independent mitigation estimators, noise-model validation on the superconducting hardware, and cross-platform corroboration at selected Floquet cycles on Quantinuum System Model H2 and Quantinuum Helios trapped-ion hardware, supports the reliability of these findings. Our work establishes error-mitigated quantum processors as quantitative scientific instruments for discovering new physics in non-equilibrium quantum matter.

</details>


### [98] [Information-theoretic limits on undetectable parameter-estimation attacks in continuous-variable quantum key distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24855)
*Agung Trisetyarso,Lenny Putri Yulianti,Kridanto Surendro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文形式化CV-QKD中的侧信道检测为证书伪造假设检验，证明了一个二分法：当散粒噪声单元可信时，漏检Stein指数为正且随Holevo信息单调递增；否则恒为零。并给出了可组合的有限码长效用分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CV-QKD的侧信道检测（如本地振荡器攻击、校准攻击）缺乏统一的信息论形式化框架，难以定量刻画检测能力与安全参数之间的关系。

Method: 将侧信道检测建模为证书伪造假设检验，利用Stein引理导出漏检指数g_M；通过单调性分析建立与Holevo信息的联系；结合有限码长分析给出非渐近边界和可组合安全密钥率公式。

Result: 1) 二分法：散粒噪声单元可信时漏检指数为正且单调递增，否则为零；2) 证书可重用率（reusability rate）作为Holevo泄漏的上界；3) 非渐近界ε_PE ≤ exp(-N_PE ψ(r))，近阈展开与高斯置信区间一致；4) 在可信相关模型下无条件成立，一般M需满足无虚假局部极小条件。

Conclusion: 建立了CV-QKD侧信道检测的信息论下界，证明了散粒噪声可信假设的关键性，为实际系统的安全参数选取提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We formalize side-channel detection in Gaussian-modulated continuous-variable quantum key distribution (CV-QKD) as a certificate-forgery hypothesis test and identify its detectability with an information-theoretic rate. Relative to a set $\mathcal{M}$ of trusted, real-time-monitored observables, the forgery- detectability rate $g_{\mathcal M}$ of a benign-statistics certificate is the missed-detection Stein exponent. We prove a dichotomy: $g_{\mathcal M}$ is monotone and strictly positive in the leaked Holevo information when the shot- noise unit is trusted, and identically zero otherwise, recovering local- oscillator and calibration attacks as the degenerate case. Monotonicity yields a certificate reusability rate bounding the Holevo leakage compatible with an undetected certificate over $N_{\mathrm{PE}}$ estimation rounds, via the non- asymptotic bound $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{PE}}\le\exp(-N_{\mathrm{PE}}ψ(r))$ whose near-threshold expansion matches the Gaussian confidence-interval scaling of standard finite-size analyses. We then give a composable finite-size key- length statement under collective Gaussian attacks, whose worst-case Holevo term is the reusability rate and whose parameter-estimation failure probability is bounded by the Stein exponent at every block length. Both results are unconditional in the trusted-correlation model, where strict monotonicity of the Holevo leakage in the excess noise follows from a noise-injection argument; for general $\mathcal{M}$ they hold under an explicit no-spurious-local-minima condition on the divergence landscape, verifiable by low-dimensional inspection. Convexity of the rate further assumes a numerically supported concavity of the Holevo bound, the sole numerical ingredient; proof status is delimited throughout.

</details>


### [99] [Numerical Modeling of Quasiparticle-Induced Dissipation in Fluxonium Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24946)
*Kate Azar,Max Hays,Kyle Serniak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过数值分析发现，通量量子比特在之前被认为对抗准粒子耗散的保护性偏置点实际上仍然存在敏感性；只有当约瑟夫森结两侧超导能隙不对称时，或光子辅助隧穿产生的准粒子具有特定能量时，才能恢复对T1的保护效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器中，由杂散红外和电离辐射产生的非平衡准粒子会限制器件性能并对量子纠错方案构成挑战。现有理论模型通常假设准粒子特征能量和量子比特跃迁能量均远小于超导能隙，在此假设下通量量子比特在特定偏置点应具有抗准粒子耗散的保护性。然而，该假设是否成立需要进一步验证。

Method: 数值分析不同准粒子能量分布下通量量子比特中准粒子诱导耗散的预测速率，包括光子辅助隧穿过程产生的准粒子情况；同时考察超导能隙不对称性对耗散速率的影响。

Result: 1) 考虑微小数值因子后，现有理论模型预测通量量子比特在之前被认为受到保护的偏置点仍会对准粒子诱导错误敏感；2) 约瑟夫森结两侧超导能隙不对称可以重新引入对准粒子诱导弛豫的抑制；3) 对于光子辅助隧穿产生的准粒子，T1保护仅在对辐射能量有特定要求时才会出现。

Conclusion: 对通量量子比特准粒子诱导耗散敏感性的理解可为通量量子比特处理器的发展和未来准粒子缓解策略提供指导。

Abstract: Nonequilibrium quasiparticles (QPs) generated by stray infrared and ionizing radiation can limit the performance of superconducting quantum processors and present challenges for quantum error correction schemes. Models of QP-induced energy relaxation commonly assume that the characteristic energy of the QPs and the qubit transition energy are both small relative to the superconducting gap. Under these assumptions, certain qubits such as the fluxonium would exhibit protection against QP-induced dissipation at specific bias points. Here, we show that this is not necessarily the case, numerically analyzing the predicted rate of QP-induced dissipation in fluxonium qubits for different QP energy distributions and for QPs created via photon-assisted tunneling processes. We find that accounting for small numerical factors, existing theoretical models predict sensitivity to QP-induced errors at bias points previously thought to be protected. We find that inclusion of asymmetry in the superconducting gap energy across the junction can reintroduce suppression of QP-induced relaxation, as expected. Additionally, for QPs created by photon-assisted tunneling, we predict that $T_1$ protection will only occur for a specific energy of pair-breaking radiation. This understanding of fluxonium sensitivity to QP-induced dissipation informs the development of fluxonium-based processors and future QP-mitigation strategies.

</details>


### [100] [Topology-dependent relativistic degradation of multipartite entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24858)
*Shu-Min Wu,Si-Han Shang,Xu Han,Hui-Chen Yang,Qianqian Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Unruh-DeWitt探测器框架下，三比特Star态中不同位置（中心或外围）的均匀加速度对量子纠缠拓扑结构的差异性影响，发现加速度位置决定了纠缠退化模式，揭示出多体量子关联的响应由Unruh热化与加速子系统的结构角色共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将相对论运动对量子纠缠的影响归因于加速度诱导的热噪声，而忽略了纠缠拓扑结构的作用。本文旨在证明，对于非对称多体纠缠态，纠缠的拓扑结构同样重要。

Method: 在Unruh-DeWitt探测器框架下，构建三比特Star态，比较两种不等价的加速配置：中心量子比特加速 vs. 外围量子比特加速。通过计算纠缠动力学（包括one-tangle和真三体纠缠）来分析拓扑依赖的纠缠退化机制。

Result: 中心加速与外围加速产生性质不同的纠缠动力学：外围加速诱导出one-tangle的复兴现象，而中心加速则无此现象；真三体纠缠虽单调衰减，但鲁棒性存在显著差异。表明加速子系统的拓扑角色决定了纠缠退化模式。

Conclusion: 多体量子关联对相对论运动的响应由Unruh热化与加速子系统的结构角色共同决定。非对称量子网络可作为操控相对论量子资源的独特平台，揭示了拓扑依赖的相对论纠缠退化新机制。

Abstract: The influence of relativistic motion on quantum entanglement is commonly attributed to acceleration-induced thermal noise. Here we show that, for asymmetric multipartite states, the topology of entanglement can become equally important. Considering a three-qubit Star state in the Unruh-DeWitt detector framework, we compare two inequivalent acceleration configurations in which either the central or a peripheral qubit undergoes uniform acceleration. We demonstrate that these physically equivalent accelerations lead to qualitatively different entanglement dynamics: acceleration of a peripheral qubit induces a revival of one-tangle that is absent when the central qubit accelerates, whereas genuine tripartite entanglement decays monotonically but with markedly different robustness. Our results uncover a topology-dependent mechanism for relativistic entanglement degradation, showing that the response of multipartite quantum correlations is determined jointly by Unruh thermalization and the structural role of the accelerated subsystem. This work identifies asymmetric quantum networks as a distinct platform for controlling relativistic quantum resources.

</details>


### [101] [Non-Hermitian entropy production from fluctuation theorems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24961)
*Vasco Cavina,Frank Ernesto Quintela Rodríguez,Donato Farina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于后选择涨落定理，为非厄米动力学建立了第一性原理热力学框架，识别出正定的熵产生量，推导了第二定律，建立了与Petz-Rényi散度的信息论联系，并将熵产生分解为非相干和相干贡献，在例外点附近发现了相干项的独特特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为非厄米量子动力学建立严格的热力学基础，特别是定义在非厄米演化过程中保持正定的熵产生量，并推导相应的第二定律。

Method: 基于后选择版本的涨落定理构建热力学框架；将所识别的熵产生量与已有的非厄米熵概念关联；通过Petz-Rényi散度建立信息论联系并推导上下界；将熵产生分解为非相干和相干两部分。

Result: 识别出在非厄米演化中保持正定的熵产生量；推导了非厄米动力学第二定律；建立了与Petz-Rényi散度的联系；在例外点附近发现相干项具有独特特征。

Conclusion: 该工作为非厄米量子系统提供了基于第一性原理的热力学解释，熵产生的分解揭示了例外点附近的量子相干效应，为理解非厄米系统的热力学行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: We develop a first-principles thermodynamic framework for non-Hermitian dynamics based on a post-selected version of the fluctuation theorem. This allows us to identify a quantity that remains positive throughout the non-Hermitian evolution and can be interpreted as the entropy production of the post-selected dynamics. We relate this quantity to previously proposed notions of non-Hermitian entropy and derive an associated second law. Furthermore, we establish a connection with information-theoretic quantities, in particular the Petz-Rényi divergences, and leverage this connection to derive upper and lower bounds. Finally, we decompose the entropy production into incoherent and coherent contributions, identifying distinctive features of the coherent term in the vicinity of exceptional points. We illustrate our results using a paradigmatic model of non-Hermitian evolution based on a two-level system.

</details>


### [102] [Fault-tolerant distributed quantum computing with a single nucleus per node](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24907)
*Yotam Vaknin,Shoham Jaocby,Roi Nevo,Aleksander Kubica,Alex Retzker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需纠缠蒸馏的分布式量子计算方案，通过工程化通信错误偏置（相位噪声主导），使纠错码自身即可净化链路，大幅降低对辅助量子比特的需求，仅需每个节点一个数据量子比特（Floquet码）或额外一个辅助比特（通用稳定子码），并提高了纠错阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算中，节点内门操作和测量保真度高，但节点间依赖低相干通信量子比特和有缺陷的光子学，导致通信连接噪声大。此前方法需消耗多个数据量子比特进行纠缠蒸馏来克服噪声，资源开销大。

Method: 通过工程化通信错误偏置，使光子贝尔对频繁发生相位错误而极少发生比特翻转错误；设计症候提取电路，使相位噪声仅表现为测量错误且不传播到数据量子比特，从而通过重复测量即可抑制，无需专用蒸馏子程序。

Result: Floquet码每个节点仅需一个数据量子比特，通用稳定子码仅需一个额外辅助比特；在该机制下实现了高纠错阈值；晶格手术在该设置下具有天然鲁棒性，其阈值接近量子存储器的阈值。

Conclusion: 量子计算机的性能受限于高质量数据量子比特，而对光子不可区分性和通信量子比特相干性的要求大幅放宽。

Abstract: Distributed quantum computing interconnects small, high-quality nodes through optical links, but this architecture carries a pronounced asymmetry: in-node gates and measurements are cheap and high-fidelity, whereas inter-node communication relies on a low-coherence communication qubit and faulty photonics. Previous approaches overcame the noisy link by placing several high-quality data qubits in each node and consuming them for Bell pair and GHZ state distillation. Here we show that distillation can be avoided altogether. The key observation is that we can engineer a communication error bias, where photonic Bell pairs suffer frequent phase errors but only rare bit-flip errors. We design the syndrome-extraction circuits so that this phase noise appears solely as a measurement error that does not propagate to the data qubits, and is therefore suppressed by simply repeating the measurement; letting the error-correcting code itself, rather than a dedicated distillation subroutine, to purify the link. This dramatically reduces the need for ancillary nuclei: Floquet codes require only a single data qubit per node, while general stabilizer codes require just one additional ancilla. We demonstrate high error-correction thresholds throughout this regime, and we identify lattice surgery as inherently robust for this setting, enabling logical operations at a threshold close to that of quantum memory. As a result, the performance of the quantum computer is limited by the high-quality data qubits, while the requirements on photon indistinguishability and coherence of the communication qubit are substantially relaxed.

</details>


### [103] [A Kernel-Based Density of States Estimator for Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24972)
*Julio Cesar Siqueira Rocha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明Rodeo算法是核多项式方法(KPM)的量子模拟，通过Haar随机输入态平均和时序采样分布，在单辅助比特量子电路上实现了态密度(DoS)的量子重构，并建立了经典信号处理窗函数与量子重构核之间的显式对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统的态密度(DoS)编码了系统的热力学和谱性质，但传统对角化方法在大Hilbert空间中不可行；经典KPM通过随机迹估计和光滑核解决此问题，而量子硬件上需要对应的有效量子算法。

Method: 将Rodeo算法与Haar随机输入态结合，通过单辅助比特量子电路对Rodeo响应进行平均，利用量子典型性抑制统计误差，并建立时序采样分布与谱核之间的显式对应关系。

Result: Rodeo算法提供了KPM的直接量子模拟：随机态扮演随机迹估计的角色，时序采样分布扮演阻尼核的角色；推导了估计量及其不确定度，建立了信号处理窗函数与量子重构核之间的显式词典，并在1D横场Ising模型和自旋-1模型上进行了数值验证。

Conclusion: Rodeo算法是一种有效的量子态密度重构方法，兼具近量子硬件的可行性（仅需标准单辅助比特电路）和随Hilbert空间维度增长的误差抑制特性，为量子多体系统的谱性质研究提供了实用的量子算法工具。

Abstract: The density of states (DoS) encodes the thermodynamic and spectral properties of quantum many-body systems, yet its reconstruction becomes intractable for Hilbert spaces too large to diagonalize. Classically, the kernel polynomial method (KPM) addresses this by combining stochastic trace estimation with a smoothing kernel. Here we show that the Rodeo algorithm---one of the simplest eigenvalue-location protocols for near-term quantum hardware---provides a direct quantum analogue of this approach. Averaging the Rodeo response over Haar-random input states yields the DoS convolved with a spectral kernel fixed entirely by the distribution of evolution times: the random states play the role of stochastic trace estimation, and the temporal sampling distribution that of the damping kernel. The construction requires only the standard single-ancilla circuit, and quantum typicality suppresses the statistical error as the Hilbert-space dimension grows. We derive the estimator and its uncertainties, establish an explicit dictionary between signal-processing window functions and quantum reconstruction kernels, and validate the method on the one-dimensional transverse-field Ising and spin-1 models.

</details>


### [104] [An algebraically closed family of informational n-qubit purity invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24987)
*Markus Frembs,Giovanni Natale,Christopher S. P. Wever,Philipp A. Hoehn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一族Pauli期望值二次型，证明它们是所有n量子比特纯态的状态无关不变量，将两量子比特的"五边形恒等式"推广到任意多量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将两量子比特情形下表征纯态空间和酉群、解释为Brukner-Zeilinger信息测度中互补性等式的"五边形恒等式"推广到任意多量子比特，因为这一推广需要新工具且能揭示两量子比特情形中不存在的结构性质。

Method: 构造一族Pauli期望值的二次型，通过引入新工具证明其对所有n量子比特纯态构成状态无关不变量，并在两篇姊妹论文中分析了这些性质与n量子比特Pauli群中互无偏性和互补性的关系。

Result: 成功将两量子比特的"五边形恒等式"推广到任意n量子比特情形，得到了更一般的Pauli期望值二次型族，并发现了新的结构性质。

Conclusion: 该族二次型是n量子比特纯态的状态无关不变量，其推广揭示了多量子比特系统中比两量子比特情形更丰富的结构性质，与Pauli群的互无偏性和互补性密切相关。

Abstract: We present a family of quadratics in Pauli expectation values, and prove that they constitute state-independent invariants for all n-qubit pure states. This family generalises the two-qubit `pentagon identities', discovered in the reconstruction programme of [P. A. Höhn, Quantum 1, 38 (2017), P. A. Höhn and C. S. P. Wever, Phys. Rev. A 95, 012102 (2017)], where they characterise the space of pure states, as well as the unitary group, and are interpreted as complementarity equalities in the Brukner-Zeilinger information measure. The generalisation to arbitrarily many qubits is nontrivial as it requires new tools which in turn reveal novel structural properties that are absent in the two-qubit case. A thorough analysis of these properties, and their relation with mutual unbiasedness and complementarity in the n-qubit Pauli group, can be found in two companion papers.

</details>


### [105] [Invariant-based master equation applied to driven qutrit coupled to a bath and a leaky cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25005)
*Sagarika Basak,A. Javadi,D. Blume*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用基于Lewis-Riesenfeld不变量的广义方法处理受驱开放N能级（N>2）量子系统的主方程，避免了传统方法对驱动强度的限制。研究表明该不变基主方程提供了一个统一的广义框架，在适当简化下可退化为实验室坐标系和旋转坐标系下的主方程。通过将两能级系统扩展为包含由一个与基态通过库诱导退相干耦合的额外态的三能级系统，发现了标准近似无法捕获的新的耗散行为。研究还将其应用于驱动量子点-漏腔耦合系统，展示了无需额外假设即可捕获相关耗散动力学的能力。该工作为存在耗散情况下的量子控制应用铺平了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主方程方法在处理驱动开放量子系统时对驱动强度有严格限制，且标准近似无法捕获某些新的耗散行为。本文旨在开发一种基于Lewis-Riesenfeld不变量的广义主方程框架，以克服这些限制并统一现有的不同主方程框架。

Method: 采用Lewis-Riesenfeld不变量构建广义主方程框架；通过对两能级系统扩展引入第三个与基态通过库诱导退相干耦合的能级；将该框架应用于驱动量子点-漏腔耦合系统进行验证。

Result: 不变基主方程可统一实验室坐标系和旋转坐标系下的主方程框架；三能级系统展现出标准近似无法捕获的新的耗散行为；该框架无需额外假设即可成功描述驱动量子点-漏腔系统的耗散动力学。

Conclusion: 所提出的不变基主方程框架克服了传统方法对驱动强度的限制，统一了不同主方程方法，并能捕获标准近似遗漏的耗散行为，为存在耗散条件下的量子控制应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: We employ a generalized approach to the master equation for driven open $N$-level ($N>2$) quantum systems using Lewis-Riesenfeld invariants, which avoids the driving-strength restrictions inherent to conventional approaches. We show that the invariant-based master equation provides a unifying generalized framework, which reduces to the frequently employed laboratory-frame master equations and the less frequently employed rotating-frame master equation framework under appropriate simplifications. Extending the prototypical two-level system, we show that the inclusion of another state coupled to the ground state via reservoir-induced dephasing gives rise to qualitatively new dissipative behaviors that are, in general, not captured by standard approximations. We also apply the invariant-based master equation framework to a driven quantum dot coupled to a leaky cavity, demonstrating the framework's ability to capture relevant dissipative dynamics without additional assumptions. Our work paves the way for quantum-control applications in the presence of dissipation.

</details>


### [106] [Maximal complementarity in the n-qubit Pauli group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24988)
*Markus Frembs,Giovanni Natale,Christopher S. P. Wever,Philipp A. Hoehn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究n量子比特系统中退化可观测量的互补性，证明了泡利群相关的可观测量具有互补性，给出了互补性判据，建立了与信息纯态互补等式的联系，并证明了这些互补集蕴含强熵不确定关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互补性是量子力学的基本原理和量子密码学的核心要素，此前强互补性主要针对非退化可观测量的素数幂维系统（如n量子比特系统），而退化可观测量的互补性尚未被充分研究。

Method: 通过分析n量子比特泡利群的可观测量的结构，推导退化可观测量互补性的判据；将最大互补集与信息纯态互补等式相联系；利用互补集推导强熵不确定关系。

Result: 得到了n量子比特泡利群中两个退化可观测量互补的判据；将最大互补集与信息纯态互补等式关联；证明了这些互补集蕴含强熵不确定关系。

Conclusion: n量子比特泡利群中的退化可观测量在强互补意义下具有互补性，这一结果可等价地表述为泡利群阿贝尔子群的最大互补集与粗粒化互无偏基的联系，并可用于推导强熵不确定关系，对量子密码学有重要意义。

Abstract: Observables in quantum mechanics are generally complementary, that is, they reveal mutually incompatible pieces of information about a given system. This property is not only a fundamental tenet of the quantum formalism, but also a key component in quantum cryptographic protocols. As such it has fuelled much research into finding sets of highly complementary observables. In its strongest form -- the one we consider in this work -- the information between complementary observables is not merely incompatible but mutually exclusive: maximal information about one observable implies no information about the other, and vice versa. Maximal sets of non-degenerate complementary observables are known to exist in systems of prime power dimension such as n-qubit systems. Here, we study complementarity of degenerate observables, specifically we prove that observables associated with the n-qubit Pauli group also exhibit complementarity under this restriction: first, we obtain a criterion for two such observables to be complementary and, second, we relate maximal sets of complementary observables with informational pure state complementarity equalities, further studied in two companion papers. Equivalently, these results can be formulated in terms of maximal complementary sets of (not necessarily maximal) Abelian subgroups of the Pauli group linking with (possibly coarse-grained) mutually unbiased bases. Finally, we prove that these complementarity sets entail strong entropic uncertainty relations.

</details>


### [107] [Detecting quantum phase transitions via shallow variational quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25345)
*Ching-Yu Huang,Min-Fong Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Delta-VQE方法在探测量子临界点时的有效性，发现使用对偶变分拟设时该方法检测到的是自对偶点而非真实临界点，只有当拟设精心设计以体现竞争相时，才能成功识别真实临界点，且仅存在微小的有限尺寸效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相图映射通过经典模拟计算代价极高，即使远离热力学极限的小系统也需要大量计算资源。变分量子本征求解器(VQE)提供了一种有前景的替代方案，其中Delta-VQE方法通过对比不同参考态的变分能量来确定临界点，且随电路深度减小而诊断效果增强。然而，该方法更广泛的适用性及其潜在机制尚不清楚，需要进一步探究。

Method: 以一维横场Ising模型结合三自旋簇相互作用为研究对象，该设定中Ising相变通常位于自对偶线之外。分别采用对偶变分拟设和精心定制的体现竞争相的拟设，比较Delta-VQE对临界点的识别效果。

Result: 当使用对偶拟设时，Delta-VQE检测到的是自对偶点而非真实临界点；而使用精心定制的体现竞争相的拟设时，能够成功识别真正的Ising临界点，仅存在微小的有限尺寸效应。

Conclusion: Delta-VQE提供了一种资源高效的量子临界性探测方法，无需精确基态制备，但其诊断能力根本上依赖于选择具有物理代表性的变分拟设。

Abstract: Mapping quantum phase diagrams through classical simulation is notoriously resource-intensive, as even small systems far from the thermodynamic limit demand prohibitive computational effort. The variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) offers a compelling alternative, exploiting approximate ground states to distinguish phases. An appealing proposal, dubbed as Delta-VQE, determines critical points by contrasting variational energies optimized from reference states of distinct phases. Intriguingly, the diagnostic sharpens as circuit depth decreases, highlighting its promise as a resource-conscious probe of quantum criticality. To probe the broader applicability and underlying mechanisms of this approach, we investigate the one-dimensional transverse-field Ising model with a three-spin cluster interaction, a setting in which the Ising transitions are generally situated beyond the self-dual line. We demonstrate that, whenever dual ansätze are employed, Delta-VQE invariably detects the self-dual points rather than the true criticality. In contrast, when ansätze are carefully tailored to embody the competing phases across the boundary, the genuine Ising critical point can be successfully identified with only minor finite-size effects. Our results establish that, while Delta-VQE provides a resource-efficient probe of quantum criticality without requiring precise ground-state preparation, its diagnostic power is fundamentally contingent upon the judicious selection of physically representative ansätze.

</details>


### [108] [Covariant Quantum Measurements and Stochastic Dynamics on Representation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25101)
*Naeem Shahid*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个群协变量子测量的理论框架，将表示空间中不可约表示扇区间的测量诱导跃迁描述为一个随机过程。通过Peter-Weyl分解和不可约张量算子构造协变测量算子，证明对于对称不变态，测量通道退化为表示图上的马尔可夫过程。利用杉浦定理约束探测器谱，并专门针对SU(2)群导出了解析形式的跃迁核、可逆性和不变测度，以及连续Fokker-Planck描述。漂移和扩散系数的解析预测与数值模拟一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量与对称性（群协变性）的结合在量子信息和基础物理中至关重要，但测量引起的动力学在表示空间上的随机过程描述尚不完善。本文旨在建立一个群协变量子测量的随机过程框架，将不可约表示扇区间的跃迁系统地刻画为表示空间上的马尔可夫过程，从而为重复协变测量提供严格的数学描述。

Method: 基于Peter-Weyl分解，从不可约张量算子构造协变测量算子；证明对称不变态下测量通道退化为表示图上的马尔可夫过程；利用杉浦定理（解析性约束）导出指数衰减探测器模型；针对SU(2)群解析求解跃迁核，证明可逆性并导出不变测度，进一步推导连续极限下的Fokker-Planck方程。

Result: 建立了表示空间上的随机过程框架；得到了SU(2)群情形下的闭式跃迁核；推导了Fokker-Planck描述中的漂移和扩散系数；理论预测与数值模拟吻合良好。

Conclusion: 本文为重复协变量子测量提供了表示空间上的随机过程描述，将群论与开量子系统动力学相结合，为对称约束下的量子测量问题提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We develop a framework for group-covariant quantum measurements in which measurement-induced transitions between irreducible representation sectors are described by a stochastic process on representation space. Starting from the Peter-Weyl decomposition, we construct covariant measurement operators from irreducible tensor operators and show that, for symmetry-invariant states, the measurement channel reduces to a Markov process on the representation graph. We further show that analyticity of the measurement operator constrains the detector spectrum through Sugiura's theorem, motivating a class of exponentially decaying detector models. Specializing to SU(2), we obtain the transition kernel in closed form, establish reversibility and the associated invariant measure, and derive a continuum Fokker-Planck description of the induced dynamics. Analytical predictions for the drift and diffusion coefficients are found to agree with numerical simulations. Our results provide a stochastic description of repeated covariant quantum measurements on representation space.

</details>


### [109] [Interaction-Endowed PT-Symmetry and its Effects on Decoherence, Einselection, and Non-Markovianity in a Central Spin Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25561)
*Leoj Phoebe M. Esquierdo,Lemuel John F. Sese,Rayda P. Gammag*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在中心自旋模型中引入PT对称性，通过可调厄米性参数γ调控非厄米相互作用。利用赝厄米形式论和Dyson映射将非厄米哈密顿量转化为厄米表示。研究发现，随着γ增大，退相干减慢，并在γ=1时完全停止。定义了与度规算符对易的赝厄米可观测量作为自哈密顿量，系统在γ→1时通过"环境诱导超选择"选择了自哈密顿量的本征态。强环境区中心自旋态的稳态纯度随γ增大呈反常下降，但存在一个对应最大信息耗散到自旋库的转折点（最小值）。BLP非马尔可夫性度量在强环境区表现出类似转折行为，信息回流达到饱和后下降，标志着PT对称性开始屏蔽中心自旋免受环境影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将PT对称性引入中心自旋模型，研究非厄米相互作用如何影响量子退相干、环境诱导超选择以及信息流动力学，探索PT对称性对开放量子系统中中心自旋的保护机制。

Method: 采用赝厄米形式论，通过Dyson映射将非厄米哈密顿量变换为等效厄米表示；定义与度规算符对易的赝厄米可观测量作为自哈密顿量；采用BLP度量量化非马尔可夫性。

Result: 退相干随γ增大而减慢，γ=1时完全停止；强环境区中心自旋稳态纯度随γ增大呈反常下降，存在转折点（最小值）；BLP度量在强环境区表现出类似的转折行为，信息回流先饱和后下降。

Conclusion: PT对称性可以有效调控中心自旋的退相干动力学，在γ=1时实现退相干完全抑制；强环境区存在信息耗散最大化的转折点，此后PT对称性开始屏蔽中心自旋；非马尔可夫信息回流与PT对称性强度存在非单调关系。

Abstract: We have introduced PT-symmetry to a central spin model by adding a PT-symmetric interaction with a tunable hermiticity parameter $γ$. Using the pseudo-Hermitian formalism, we applied a Dyson map to transform the non-Hermitian Hamiltonian to its Hermitian representation. We have found that the decoherence slows down as $γ$ increases, eventually ceasing at $γ=1$. We define a pseudo-Hermitian observable that commutes with the metric operator to add as the self-Hamiltonian. Einselection gradually forced the system to select the eigenstates of the self-Hamiltonian as $γ\rightarrow1$. The steady-state purity of the central spin states in the strong environment regime exhibits a paradoxical decrease as $γ$ increases. However, a turning point (minimum) corresponding to a maximum information dissipation to the spin bath is found. Finally, the Breuer-Laine-Piilo (BLP) measure, which is used to quantify the non-Markovianity of a quantum system, was evaluated for a finite time. The BLP measure in the strong environment regime exhibited similar turning point behavior, which means that the information backflow reaches a saturation point before declining. This decline signifies the point where PT-symmetry starts shielding the central spin from the environment.

</details>


### [110] [Realizing Error Suppression in Partially Fault-Tolerant Quantum Simulations with IBM Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24947)
*Henry Froland,Dorota M. Grabowska,Sebastian Grieninger,Jeremy Hartse,Anne L. Lashbrook,Zhiyao Li,Ziyuan Li,Sarah J. M. Powell,Martin J. Savage,Xiaojun Yao,Nikita A. Zemlevskiy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在IBM量子计算机ibm_boston上实验演示了使用[[4,2,2]] Iceberg码进行部分容错编码量子模拟，在1+1D和2+1D伊辛模型中，编码方案相比未编码基线将局域可观测量精度提升了2-200%以上，并提出了基于综合征相关性的选择性后选择技术Observable-Ranked Postselection以减少资源开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码有望提升含噪量子设备上量子模拟的性能，但完全容错的逻辑门集通常需要极高的开销。本文旨在探索一种折中方案，在避免完全容错高开销的同时，通过部分容错的编码来提升近期量子模拟的精度。

Method: 使用[[4,2,2]] Iceberg码将42个逻辑量子比特映射到ibm_boston的136个物理量子比特上，结合容错综合征提取与非容错逻辑操作；利用编码的方形逻辑连接性和块内逻辑量子比特放置自由度来降低2D模拟的电路深度；提出Observable-Ranked Postselection技术，基于综合征相关性进行选择性过滤，而非完全的后选择。

Result: 在1+1D模拟中，编码方案在中等时间步将局域可观测量精度提升2-6%，并随电路深度增长；在2+1D模拟中，在最后时间步精度提升超过200%。

Conclusion: 部分容错的编码量子模拟方案能够在近期含噪量子硬件上有效提升模拟精度，且Observable-Ranked Postselection技术可在不损失过多样本的情况下恢复可靠结果，为近期的容错量子模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum error-detecting codes offer a near-term path for improving the performance of quantum simulations on noisy hardware. Using IBM's superconducting quantum computer ibm_boston, we show that partially fault-tolerant encoded quantum simulations of the Ising model in 1+1D and 2+1D outperform their unencoded counterparts in estimating local observables. To represent 42 logical qubits on the heavy-hex quantum processor, 21 blocks of the [[4, 2, 2]] Iceberg code and up to 136 physical qubits are used. By pairing fault-tolerant syndrome extraction with non-fault-tolerant logical operations, this scheme preserves many of the benefits of error detection while avoiding the overhead typically required for a fully fault-tolerant logical gate set. The encoding's square logical connectivity, together with the freedom to place logical qubits within each block, enables simulations of a 2D spatial lattice with lower circuit depth than the unencoded implementation requires. We introduce Observable-Ranked Postselection, a selective-filtering technique based on syndrome correlations that recovers reliable results without the prohibitive shot loss of full syndrome postselection. Under the cumulative effect of device errors, this encoding improves local-observable accuracy over the unencoded baseline by 2-6% at intermediate times in 1+1D simulations, growing with circuit depth to over 200% in 2+1D at the latest times studied.

</details>


### [111] [Discrete power-law decay of subsystem distance after a quantum quench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25661)
*Bin Sui,Jiaju Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过数值研究一维无限横向场Ising链中的全局量子淬火，利用Bures距离量化子系统从随时间演化的约化密度矩阵向稳态广义Gibbs系综的偏离。发现后期衰减遵循离散幂律B_A(t) ~ t^{-λ}，指数λ取离散值（1,5/4,3/2,7/4,2,5/2等），由淬火前后横向场及对称激发分数函数的连续性、边界值及一阶导数边界值等性质共同决定，揭示了可积量子系统中局部平衡动力学的普适离散结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解可积量子系统在全局淬火后子系统向稳态弛豫的动力学机制，特别是局部可观测量的平衡化过程及其中可能存在的普适结构。

Method: 采用Bures距离量化子系统约化密度矩阵与广义Gibbs系综（GGE）稳态的偏差，通过数值求解无限一维横向场Ising链中的全局量子淬火过程，分析对称激发分数函数的性质对衰减指数的影响。

Result: 发现Bures距离的后期衰减遵循离散幂律，指数λ取值为1, 5/4, 3/2, 7/4, 2, 5/2等离散值。衰减指数由淬火前后横向场参数以及对称激发分数函数的连续性、边界值和一阶导数边界值共同决定。初始基态淬火对应的t^{-3/2}衰减作为特例被自然恢复。

Conclusion: 可积量子系统中子系统局部平衡化动力学存在普适的离散幂律结构，揭示了Bures距离作为度量工具在刻画量子弛豫中的有效性。

Abstract: We present a numerical study of subsystem distance decay following a global quantum quench in the infinite one-dimensional transverse-field Ising chain, using the mathematically rigorous Bures distance $B_A(t)$ to quantify the deviation of the time-evolved reduced density matrix from its stationary generalized Gibbs ensemble state. We show that the late-time decay follows a discrete power law $B_A(t) \sim t^{-λ}$, with the exponent $λ$ confined to discrete values: $1$, $5/4$, $3/2$, $7/4$, $2$, $5/2$, and potentially further values. The specific exponent is jointly determined by the pre- and post-quench transverse fields, as well as by properties of the symmetric excitation-fraction function $m_S(\varphi)$, defined on $\varphi\in[0,π]$ to characterize the pre-quench Hamiltonian eigenstates, including continuity, boundary values, and first-derivative boundary values, among others. The previously established $t^{-3/2}$ decay for the initial ground state of the pre-quench Hamiltonian is naturally recovered as a special case of this general classification. Our results reveal a universal discrete structure governing local equilibration dynamics in integrable quantum systems.

</details>


### [112] [Landau Levels on the Surface of a Cube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25684)
*Almila Kura,Bayram Tekin,Mehmet Özgür Oktel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究立方体表面约束带电粒子在磁单极子场中的量子力学问题，通过规范修正式旋转算子分类本征态，发现偶数磁荷对应立方旋转群O的不可约表示，奇数磁荷需二元八面体群2O的旋量表示，并用规范协变有限差分法计算了类似朗道能级的谱结构及Hofstadter问题中的角态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在具有离散对称性（立方体）的紧致表面上，磁单极子场与带电粒子相互作用的量子力学行为，特别是规范结构与对称性之间的关系。

Method: 使用两个规范片覆盖立方体表面建立连续模型；构造规范修正式旋转算子进行对称性分类；采用规范协变有限差分法离散化求解本征谱；研究对应的紧束缚Hofstadter模型。

Result: 狄拉克量子化条件自然导出；偶数/奇数磁荷分别对应O群/2O群的表示；能谱呈现类似朗道能级的简并流形被立方对称性分裂的结构；Hofstadter谱中出现位于立方体角附近的局域隙态。

Conclusion: 立方体表面磁单极子问题在连续和离散框架下均表现出丰富的对称性约束谱结构，为研究拓扑缺陷与离散对称性的相互作用提供了新范例。

Abstract: We study the quantum mechanics of a charged particle confined to the surface of a cube enclosing a magnetic monopole. The magnetic field is chosen to have a constant magnitude on each face and to point along the outward normal, preserving the rotational symmetry of the cube. We formulate the continuum problem using two gauge patches on the cube surface and show that consistency of the wavefunction gives the Dirac quantization condition. Since an explicit vector potential does not remain invariant under ordinary rotations, we construct gauge-modified rotation operators and use them to classify the eigenstates. Even monopole charges are described by the irreducible representations of the cubic rotation group O, while odd monopole charges require the spinorial representations of the binary octahedral group 2O. We compute the spectrum with a gauge-covariant finite-difference discretization and find Landau-level-like manifolds whose degeneracies are split by the discrete cubic symmetry. We also study the corresponding tight-binding Hofstadter problem on the discretized cube. The resulting spectrum contains the usual magnetic subband structure together with additional gap states localized near the cube corners.

</details>


### [113] [Universal Optimization and Tighter Fidelity Bounds for Approximate Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24968)
*Jing Wu,Michele Grossi,Doga Kurkcuoglu,Silvia Zorzetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于恢复与环境退耦对偶性的近似量子纠错（AQEC）高效框架，通过主成分分析降维噪声Kraus算子，在热损耗信道中实现33倍计算加速，同时保持严格精度，突破了传统半定规划在大规模AQEC优化中的维度诅咒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AQEC中通过半定规划优化恢复信道以最大化纠缠保真度，其计算瓶颈在于Kraus算子数量随系统规模指数增长，导致大规模优化不可行。现有解析近似映射仅在Knill-Laflamme条件近似满足时有效，缺乏通用高效的计算框架。

Method: 1. 建立恢复信道与环境退耦之间的对偶性，推导出比传统转置信道更紧的纠缠保真度解析下界；2. 利用噪声Kraus算子的权重衰减特性，引入主成分分析（PCA）对Kraus算子空间进行降维，从而缩减优化问题的规模。

Result: 在热损耗信道（噪声权重指数衰减）中，该方法实现33倍计算加速，同时保持严格的数值精度，使得此前因维度诅咒而难以处理的大规模AQEC码优化成为可能。

Conclusion: 该框架通过理论对偶性和数据驱动降维的双重策略，为大规模AQEC码的高精度优化提供了一条可行路径，拓展了AQEC在各类物理系统中的可应用性。

Abstract: Approximate quantum error correction (AQEC) not only dictates the performance of discrete- and continuous-variable quantum error correction codes but also serves as a unifying framework across various physical disciplines. Identifying the optimal recovery channel to maximize the entanglement fidelity via standard semidefinite programming is computationally bottlenecked by the exponentially growing number of Kraus operators with system size, rendering large-scale optimization prohibitive. While analytical near-optimal maps exist, they typically work only when the Knill-Laflamme conditions are nearly satisfied. In this Letter, we establish an efficient framework by leveraging the duality between recovery and environment decoupling. This framework yields a tighter analytical lower bound on entanglement fidelity than the conventional limit set by the transpose channel. Furthermore, by exploiting the decayed weights of noise Kraus operators, we introduce a framework based on principal component analysis to reduce the dimension. In thermal loss channels where the weights decay exponentially, our approach achieves a 33-fold computational speedup while maintaining rigorous accuracy. Our approach enables high-precision optimization for AQEC codes that were previously intractable due to the curse of dimensionality.

</details>


### [114] [Lowering the implementation barrier of neutral-atom quantum computing with agentic workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25834)
*Constantin Dalyac,Alexandre Dauphin,Loïc Henriet,Christophe Jurczak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化量子计算流水线的智能体工作流，能够将理论论文或专利自动转化为中性原子量子处理器上的硬件实验，并通过三个案例展示了其在多体物理和优化问题中的应用。同时发现人类专家对科学有效性的验证至关重要，因为智能体可能选择不当观测值或给出错误诊断。此外，通过第二个智能体对633篇Rydberg阵列论文的分类表明，近半数可在当前量子处理器上实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将理论量子协议转化为硬件实验存在巨大瓶颈，需要协议设计、编译、模拟和云执行等多领域专业知识，自动化这一流程可显著降低量子实验的门槛。

Method: 设计了一个智能体工作流，自动完成从理论论文/专利到中性原子量子处理器（Pasqal QPU）实验的全流程，同时保留人类专家对关键环节的验证。另用一个分类智能体对633篇arXiv论文进行可实施性评估。

Result: 智能体在三个案例中成功从论文/专利出发，自动完成QPU实验；但人类评审发现两处错误：一例选择了不恰当的可观测值，另一例给出看似合理但错误的硬件诊断。论文分类结果显示近半数可在当前硬件上实现。

Conclusion: 智能体工作流为理论想法与物理硬件之间提供了实际桥梁，可大幅扩展量子实验的可及性，但人类专家在科学有效性验证方面仍不可或缺。

Abstract: Quantum computers are moving from research laboratories to industrial machines accessible via the cloud and integrated into high-performance computing facilities. However, translating theoretical quantum protocols into hardware experiments remains a major bottleneck, requiring expertise across protocol design, compilation, simulation, and cloud execution. Here, we introduce an agentic workflow that automates this pipeline on neutral-atom quantum processors (here two Pasqal QPUs available on the cloud) while keeping the researcher in the loop for critical validation. In three case studies from many-body physics and optimization, the agent went from published paper or patent to a QPU campaign run overnight. In particular, human intervention was crucial to ensure scientific validity: the agent selected an inadequate observable in one experiment and constructed a plausible but incorrect hardware diagnosis in another, with both failures detected only through domain-expert review. Finally, we use a second agent to classify a corpus of 633 Rydberg-array arXiv papers and show that nearly half are implementable on present-day QPUs while identifying specific hardware upgrades needed for the rest. Together, these results demonstrate that agentic workflows provide a practical bridge between theoretical ideas and physical hardware, opening quantum experimentation to a much broader scientific community.

</details>


### [115] [Quantum reference frames beyond subsystems: a reconstruction and generalization of the perspective-neutral framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24976)
*Stefan L. Ludescher,Manuel Mekonnen,Thomas D. Galley,Markus P. Mueller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子参考系（QRF）的概念从张量因子子系统推广到协变量子仪器，涵盖了不可区分粒子的标记框架、关系时钟等物理应用，并推广了Höhn等人开创的视角中性方法，为约束量子化提供了操作澄清。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子参考系框架通常将参考系视为张量因子子系统，限制了其在不可区分粒子、关系时钟等场景中的应用。本文旨在通过协变量子仪器这一更一般的数学结构来统一和扩展QRF框架。

Method: 将QRF定义为协变量子仪器而非张量因子子系统；从简单操作场景重构Höhn等人的视角中性方法；引入完全协变操作的概念；给出该框架的资源理论基础；将关系化映射推广到仪器层面。

Result: 实现了不可区分粒子的"标记框架"，解释了对称化公设和不存在parastatistics的原因；给出了玻色子和费米子纠缠的透明描述；构建了即使所有子系统相互作用或存在频率超选择定则时也能精确再现薛定谔方程的关系时钟；阐释了约束方程$C|ψ\rangle=0$的操作意义。

Conclusion: 本文的工作架起了操作量子信息理论与内部QRF研究项目之间的桥梁，其关系化映射推广到仪器层面的结果具有更广泛的适用性，为约束量子化提供了操作层面的澄清。

Abstract: We generalize the notion of quantum reference frames (QRFs) to cases where the frame does not necessarily correspond to a tensor factor subsystem, but to a covariant quantum instrument. This unlocks a variety of physical applications: "frames of labeling" for indistinguishable particles, suggesting explanations for the symmetrization postulate and the absence of parastatistics, and yielding a transparent description of the entanglement of bosons and fermions; and relational clocks reproducing the Schrödinger equation exactly even when all subsystems are interacting or when there are frequency superselection sectors. Our work generalizes the perspective-neutral approach to QRFs pioneered by Höhn and co-authors, which we reconstruct from a simple operational scenario. We give a resource-theoretic grounding of this framework, and show how the notion of completely covariant operations explains the relevance of the charge-zero sector and the pure-state transformation behavior across perspectives. This also suggests operational clarifications of some aspects of constraint quantization, e.g. of the meaning of constraint equations such as $C|ψ\rangle=0$. Some of our results, such as our generalization of relationalization maps to instruments, apply more broadly to other QRF frameworks too, and they contribute to bridging the gap between operational quantum information theory and the internal QRF research program.

</details>


### [116] [Path integral approach to the truncated Wigner approximation of driven-dissipative spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26029)
*Viktoria Noel,Michael Fleischhauer,Igor Lesanovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为开放量子自旋-1/2系统建立了截断维格纳近似（TWA）的Keldysh路径积分表述，将玻色子系统中已知的场论方法推广到自旋系统，并证明正确处理耗散需要将算符乘积映射到弯曲的自旋相空间，从而为耗散自旋动力学的TWA提供了统一的场论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将玻色子系统中TWA与Keldysh路径积分截断等价的场论方法推广到相互作用的自旋-1/2系统，为自旋系统的半经典模拟提供严格的场论基础。

Method: 利用连续的SU(2)相空间，建立开放相互作用自旋-1/2系统的Keldysh路径积分表述，在量子涨落的二阶截断下推导出随机运动方程，并与连续TWA公式进行对比验证。

Result: 证明了正确处理耗散需要将算符乘积一致地映射到弯曲的自旋相空间，得到的随机方程与连续TWA公式一致，并且能够正确重现单个耗散自旋的精确动力学。

Conclusion: 为耗散自旋动力学的TWA建立了统一的场论基础，为超越半经典近似的扩展提供了系统性的出发点。

Abstract: Phase-space approaches such as the truncated Wigner approximation (TWA) provide an efficient semiclassical framework for performing approximate simulations of the dynamics of open quantum many-body systems outside the reach of exact numerical methods but beyond the mean-field level. For bosonic systems, TWA is known to be equivalent to a Keldysh path-integral formulation truncated at second order in the so-called quantum fluctuations. This semiclassical approach provides an alternative transparent route towards approximate stochastic equations of motion, which can be efficiently solved. Here we establish the corresponding path-integral formulation for interacting open spin-$1/2$ systems using the continuous $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ phase space. We show, in particular, that a consistent treatment of dissipation requires correctly mapping operator products onto the curved spin phase space, leading to stochastic equations that coincide with those obtained from the continuous TWA formulation and thus reproduce the exact dynamics of a single dissipative spin. Our results provide a unified field-theoretic foundation for the TWA to dissipative spin dynamics and offer a systematic starting point for extensions beyond the semiclassical approximation.

</details>


### [117] [A Model Predictive Control-Inspired Quantum Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24992)
*Dominic Messina,Alicia B. Magann,Mohan Sarovar,Helen Durand*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种受模型预测控制（MPC）启发的混合量子-经典算法，该算法统一了变分量子算法（VQA）的优化设计框架与反馈型量子算法（FQA）的反馈设计框架，通过逐层滚动时域策略优化变分电路参数，并证明其性能至少不亚于FQA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变分量子算法（VQA）和反馈型量子算法（FQA）各有优势但设计框架分离，缺乏一种能够统一二者优化与反馈机制的算法，同时希望利用高性能计算提升量子计算中动态预测的规模和效率。

Method: 提出基于模型预测控制（MPC）的混合量子-经典算法：采用逐层滚动时域优化策略，每一层量子门操作后的可观测量测量结果用于初始化一个经典模拟的动态模型，该模型预测量子态演化，并据此优化未来参数化量子门。

Result: 通过数学证明和数值实验（Max-Cut问题和二维横向场Ising模型）表明，该MPC算法在理论上至少能达到FQA的性能，在实践中采用宽松实现方案时还能获得比FQA更优的性能表现。

Conclusion: MPC算法统一了VQA与FQA的设计范式，为量子计算机与高性能计算的集成提供了理想的应用场景，有望在基态制备和组合优化等任务中带来性能提升。

Abstract: We introduce a new hybrid quantum-classical algorithm inspired by an advanced control strategy known as model predictive control (MPC). This algorithm unifies the optimization-based design of variational quantum algorithms (VQAs) with the feedback-based design of feedback-based quantum algorithms (FQAs). Variational circuit parameters are optimized using a layer-wise receding horizon strategy, where observable measurements after every layer initialize a classically simulated dynamic model used to predict quantum state evolution and optimize over future parameterized gates. This hybrid algorithm can be used for applications such as ground state preparation and approximate combinatorial optimization, and presents an ideal use case for the integration of quantum computers with high-performance computing, where the latter resource can be used to increase the scale and efficiency of the predictions critical to MPC. We show through mathematical proof and numerical evidence that the MPC-based algorithm can be guaranteed to at least match the performance of FQAs. Through simulations on Max-Cut problems and a two-dimensional transverse-field Ising model, we demonstrate that relaxed implementations of the MPC-based algorithm can also provide improved performance in practice compared to an FQA.

</details>


### [118] [A broadband, individually addressing two- and three-dimensional photonic integrated circuit for trapped-ion qubit control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25062)
*Daniel Klawson,Yiyang Zhi,Bingran You,Michael Bareian,Elijah Mossman,Chun-Yuan Fan,Arkadev Roy,Ke Sun,Jason Lee,Sung Cheol Yoon,Qiming Wu,Lai Jiang,Wenjun Ke,Weiwei Wu,Sirui Tang,Zachary Wall,Jiaxiang Wang,Louis Paul Romero,Sam Vizvary,Steven Diaz,Eric R. Hudson,Wesley C. Campbell,Hartmut Haeffner,Ming C. Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种基于光子集成芯片的宽带离子寻址方案，通过结合平面波导透镜和双光子聚合3D打印微镜，实现了在405-880 nm波段对单个离子的低串扰寻址，并成功捕获和操控钙离子与钡离子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统囚禁离子量子计算中，多波长光路的自由空间传输方案缺乏可扩展性，而片上光栅耦合器带宽窄、占地面积大、光学接口复杂，制约了大规模离子网络的发展。

Method: 将平面波导透镜与通过晶圆级双光子聚合技术制造的微镜相结合，构成宽带光子集成电路；在器件上方囚禁40Ca⁺和138Ba⁺离子进行验证。

Result: 实现了405-880 nm宽带内对间距5 μm的三个独立离子的寻址，平均强度串扰为-27 dB；成功囚禁两种离子，表征了光学串扰，并演示了对钙离子的独立重泵浦操作。

Conclusion: 该单片光子架构将宽带光寻址能力集成到片上平台，推动了囚禁离子技术的可扩展性；同时表明将增材制造融入量子器件可拓展新颖量子架构的设计空间。

Abstract: Trapped ions provide a high-fidelity platform for quantum information processing, yet delivery of multiple, distinct wavelengths across large networks of interaction zones remains a bottleneck. Conventional free-space light delivery lacks scalability, while on-chip grating couplers suffer from narrow operational bandwidth that increases circuit footprint and optical interfacing complexity. Here we show a broadband photonic integrated circuit capable of addressing individual ions. The circuit combines a planar waveguide lens with a micromirror fabricated using two-photon polymerization at wafer scale. This implementation can address three individual ions from $λ$ = 405 - 880 nm with -27 dB average intensity crosstalk at $5\,μ\mathrm{m}$ pitch. We trap $^{40}\mathrm{Ca}^{+}$ and $^{138}\mathrm{Ba}^{+}$ ions above such devices, characterize optical crosstalk with barium ions, and demonstrate individual repumping of calcium ions. This monolithic photonic architecture brings broadband addressing in an on-chip modality to trapped-ion technology. More generally, integrating additive manufacturing into quantum devices is poised to unlock expanded design space for implementing novel quantum architectures.

</details>


### [119] [Exceptional-point braiding with native controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25109)
*Vishnu Chavva,Nero Osmancevic,Hugo Ribeiro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对非厄米系统奇异点编织的约束型捷径绝热方法，仅使用原生控制（失谐量、驱动振幅）即可实现理想绝热分支交换的量子态转移，并通过耗散transmon实验验证了其鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异点通过全纯延拓实现本征态的分支交换，但实际的动态实现面临困难：慢速环绕无法传输全部瞬时本征态，而传统的捷径绝热方法需要超出实验天然可控范围的操控手段。

Method: 在修饰的瞬时本征态框架下，利用可用控制仅在局域上抵消可访问的跃迁通道，残余通道在演化中活跃但在闭合回路上无净累积效应。在最小二模非厄米模型（等效于耗散transmon实验的有效哈密顿量）中，仅通过光滑实值波形调整失谐量和驱动振幅实现。

Result: 该方法再现了分支交换转移，并在标定误差、奇异点不确定性和有限带宽滤波下保持准确性，只有中等程度的额外开销。

Conclusion: 提出了一种约束型捷径绝热原理，利用原生控制实现奇异点编织中的量子态转移，无需完全局域抵消或全程绝热跟随，且对实验误差具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Exceptional points define branch-exchange state transfers through holomorphic continuation of non-Hermitian eigenmodes, but realizing these transfers dynamically remains difficult. Slow encircling does not generally transport the full set of instantaneous eigenstates, while shortcuts to adiabaticity can require controls outside the native experimental manifold. Here, we introduce a constrained shortcut-to-adiabaticity principle for exceptional-point braiding using native controls. In a dressed instantaneous-eigenstate frame, the available controls cancel the accessible transition channels locally, while the residual channels remain active during the evolution but are constrained to have no net accumulated effect over the closed loop. The protocol therefore targets the endpoint state transfer selected by ideal adiabatic branch exchange, rather than enforcing complete local cancellation or adiabatic following throughout the trajectory. We demonstrate the construction in a minimal two-mode non-Hermitian model equivalent, up to a trace shift and a basis convention, to the effective Hamiltonian used in dissipative-transmon exceptional-point experiments, where detuning and drive amplitude provide real controls and the relative loss imbalance fixes the non-Hermitian scale. Smooth real waveforms reshape only these controls, reproduce the branch-exchange transfer, and remain accurate under calibration errors, exceptional-point uncertainty and finite-bandwidth filtering with modest overhead.

</details>


### [120] [Agentic AI for Scientific Reasoning in Autonomous Quantum Sensing Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25145)
*Takuya Isogawa,Ryotaro Okabe,Nutdech Phadetsuwannukun,Mingda Li,Paola Cappellaro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于大语言模型（LLM）代理的自主量子实验工作流，用于金刚石氮-空位（NV）中心的量子传感实验。主要贡献包括：1）演示了完整的自主NV实验流程（包括中心选择、频率校准、T₂*测量及CPMG脉冲序列的添加）；2）提出了两个离线基准测试（Ramsey检查点和pODMR数据评估），用于独立评估代理的推理能力。实验表明，更高的推理努力在Ramsey基准中有助于发现残余校准偏差，但在pODMR基准中导致更多误报，而引入预期信号计算可有效降低误报率。研究建议自主实验应采用明确的劳动分工：代理负责科学假设和定量数据分析，确定性代码负责硬件控制和安全性保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NV中心是量子传感的广泛使用的平台，其计算机控制的测量特性为自主实验工作流提供了天然场景。当前缺乏能够独立进行科学推理、实验决策与数据评估的自动化系统，本文旨在开发并评估基于LLM代理的自主实验框架。

Method: 实现了一个LLM代理驱动的自主实验工作流，结合持久化项目记录、定量计算与数据分析工具及确定性实验控制。同时设计了两个离线基准测试（Ramsey检查点和pODMR数据评估），使用GPT-5.4、GPT-5.5和GPT-5.6 Sol评估代理在不同推理努力水平下的表现。

Result: 在自主实验中，代理成功完成NV中心选择、共振频率校准、Ramsey测量T₂*以及添加CPMG测量以检查可能的¹³C相关弱特征。基准测试结果显示：Ramsey基准中更高推理努力有助于识别共振校准偏移；pODMR基准中仅依赖脉冲序列信息时更高推理努力导致更多误报，而加入预期信号计算后误报率在所有模型和推理设置下保持较低水平。

Conclusion: 自主实验应采用明确的劳动分工：LLM代理负责形成科学假设并使用定量工具评估数据，确定性代码控制硬件并执行安全约束。这种分工在提高自主实验可靠性的同时充分发挥了LLM的推理能力。

Abstract: We implement an agentic AI workflow built around a large language model (LLM) agent for autonomous experiments with nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond. NV centers are a widely used platform for quantum sensing, and the ability to control many measurements from a computer makes NV experiments a natural setting for autonomous workflows. We make two main contributions. First, we demonstrate an autonomous NV experiment workflow that combines persistent project records, quantitative calculation and data analysis tools, and deterministic experiment control. In one autonomous experiment, the agent selected a single NV center, calibrated its resonant frequency, measured \(T_2^\ast\) with Ramsey measurements, and added a Carr--Purcell--Meiboom--Gill (CPMG) measurement to check a weak feature that could be related to nearby \(^{13}\mathrm{C}\). Second, we introduce two offline benchmarks that evaluate the agent's reasoning separately from laboratory execution. We evaluated both benchmarks with GPT-5.4, GPT-5.5, and GPT-5.6 Sol. In the Ramsey checkpoint benchmark, greater reasoning effort generally improved recognition of a residual resonance calibration offset. By contrast, in the pulsed optically detected magnetic resonance (pODMR) data evaluation benchmark, pulse sequence information alone produced more false positive resonance judgments at higher reasoning effort. Requiring an expected signal calculation kept false positive rates low across all three models and reasoning settings. The results suggest a clear division of labor for autonomous experiments. The agent forms scientific hypotheses and uses quantitative tools to evaluate data, while deterministic code controls the hardware and enforces safety constraints.

</details>


### [121] [Quantum Transformer BSDE Solver via Multi-Layer Fully-Connected Variational Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25162)
*Howard Su,Huan-Hsin Tseng,Chi-Sheng Chen,Lance Bai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多层全连接变分量子电路的量子Transformer BSDE求解器，用于求解高维半线性抛物型偏微分方程。该方法将归一化状态轨迹作为时间-坐标令牌，通过因果自注意力机制学习BSDE梯度过程中的交互，所有可训练参数均包含在量子电路中。在d=36的三个PDE基准测试中，量子Transformer优于非注意力基线，并在紧凑隐藏宽度下超越经典Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维抛物型偏微分方程在工程、物理和随机控制等领域具有重要应用，但传统数值方法面临维度灾难。深度BSDE方法虽提供了求解途径，但现有方法的效率和精度仍有提升空间，尤其在量子计算与深度学习结合方面尚缺乏有效探索。

Method: 提出量子Transformer BSDE求解器，核心组件为多层全连接变分量子电路（FC-VQC）。将归一化状态轨迹视为时间-坐标令牌，采用因果自注意力机制捕捉BSDE梯度过程中的序列依赖关系。可训练参数全部嵌入FC-VQC的嵌入、投影、前馈和解码模块，注意力及结构性操作保持经典且无参数。

Result: 在维度d=36的三个PDE基准测试上，量子Transformer一致优于非注意力FC-VQC基线；在紧凑隐藏宽度下超越经典Transformer；宽隐藏宽度的经典Transformer在整体精度上最优。

Conclusion: 将因果注意力与FC-VQC相结合为高维BSDE轨迹学习提供了一种有效的量子架构，验证了量子-经典混合方法在PDE求解中的可行性。

Abstract: Solving high-dimensional parabolic partial differential equations (PDEs) is important in engineering, physics, and stochastic control. Deep BSDE methods reformulate semilinear PDEs as backward stochastic differential equations and admit a model-based reinforcement learning interpretation, where trajectories are generated from known stochastic dynamics while a trainable model learns the gradient-related control process. We propose a Quantum Transformer BSDE solver based on Multi-Layer Fully-Connected Variational Quantum Circuits (FC-VQC). The method treats the normalized state trajectory as time--coordinate tokens and applies causal self-attention to learn interactions in the adapted BSDE gradient process. All trainable model parameters are contained within the FC-VQC embedding, projection, feed-forward, and decoder modules, while attention and structural operations remain classical and parameter-free. Experiments on three d=36 PDE benchmarks show that QTransformer consistently improves over the non-attentive FC-VQC baseline and outperforms the classical Transformer at compact hidden widths, while the wider classical Transformer achieves the best overall accuracy. These results demonstrate that combining causal attention with FC-VQC provides an effective quantum architecture for high-dimensional BSDE trajectory learning.

</details>


### [122] [QUBO-Based Optimization of Social Indicator Configurations for Working-Age Population Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25170)
*Hayate Wada,Seiya Miyamoto,Tatsumasa Ogawa,Kazuki Uehara,Masaru Hitomi,Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于QUBO（二次无约束二元优化）的框架，利用日本市政数据探索与劳动年龄人口增长相关的社会指标配置。通过将2010-2020年劳动年龄人口增长率回归到十个离散化的社会指标上，构建了具有良好预测性能的二次代理模型（测试集相关系数0.84，平均R²值0.76），并使用量子退火、模拟退火和Gurobi三种方法求解最优配置。该框架提供了一种可解释且可优化的方法，用于连接市政社会统计、非线性交互和基于模型的场景生成，作为政策讨论的探索性工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 劳动年龄人口下降是区域可持续性的重大挑战，尤其在日本等老龄化社会。现有方法难以有效捕捉社会指标间的非线性交互作用，并缺乏将统计建模与优化求解相结合的框架，因此需要一种可解释且优化就绪的方法来探索社会指标配置与人口增长之间的关系。

Method: 利用日本市政数据，将2010-2020年劳动年龄人口增长率对十个离散化社会指标进行二次回归，构建代理模型；将拟合模型转化为带one-hot约束的QUBO形式，分别使用量子退火、模拟退火和Gurobi求解最优配置；并进行单一指标层面的分析以探究指标间的非线性交互效应。

Result: 二次代理模型测试集相关系数达0.84，平均R²值为0.76；三种优化方法均识别出相同的最优可行配置，退火采样器还生成了具有不同预测增长率的可行次优配置；单一指标分析显示，改变一个指标的影响取决于其他指标的取值，揭示了指标间的非线性交互作用。

Conclusion: 该QUBO框架提供了一种可解释、优化就绪的探索性工具，能够将市政社会统计、非线性交互和基于模型的场景生成有效连接，适用于政策讨论中的情景分析，但不应作为政策干预的因果估计。

Abstract: The decline of the working-age population is a major challenge for regional sustainability, particularly in ageing societies such as Japan. We present a methodological demonstration of a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO)-based framework for exploring social-indicator configurations associated with working-age population growth. Using Japanese municipal data, we regressed the 2010-2020 working-age population growth rate on ten discretized social indicators. The resulting quadratic surrogate model showed reasonable predictive performance, with a test-set correlation coefficient of 0.84 and an average R-squared value of 0.76. Its coefficient matrix provides an interpretable representation of individual indicator-level contributions and pairwise associations. We converted the fitted model into a QUBO formulation with one-hot constraints and optimized it using quantum annealing, simulated annealing, and Gurobi. All three methods identified the same optimal feasible configuration, while the annealing-based samplers also generated feasible suboptimal configurations with different predicted growth rates. Municipality-level single-indicator analyses showed that changing one indicator can increase or decrease the predicted growth rate depending on the other indicators. The framework provides an interpretable and optimization-ready approach for connecting municipal social statistics, nonlinear interactions, and model-based scenario generation. It should be regarded as an exploratory tool for policy discussion rather than as a causal estimate of policy interventions.

</details>


### [123] [Dissipation Enables Strongly Detuning-Dependent Interference in Pulsed Dynamical Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25176)
*Kenneth DeRose,Jonah Glick,Kefeng Jiang,Hans Johnson,Tanay Roy,Sharika Saraf,Anya Abraham,Hardeep Singh,Tim Kovachy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现耗散（激发态衰变）会根本上改变脉冲动力学去耦的行为，使原本对静态失谐不敏感的量子比特产生强烈的失谐依赖性干涉信号，并基于此开发了耗散Carr-Purcell光谱学（DCPS）新技术，在锶原子干涉仪和超导transmon量子比特中进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲动力学去耦通常能抑制驱动场与量子比特共振之间的静态失谐误差，但耗散效应可能改变这一行为。作者旨在研究耗散（激发态衰变）如何影响脉冲动力学去耦，并探索其潜在应用价值。

Method: 建立了解析和数值模型来捕捉耗散引起的失谐依赖性机制，并在自由空间锶原子干涉仪和超导transmon量子比特两个实验平台上进行了验证。

Result: 发现激发态衰变会产生强烈的失谐依赖性干涉信号，该信号源于驱动演化过程中的衰变事件以及衰变后量子比特保持的相干性。基于此发展了DCPS光谱学技术。

Conclusion: 耗散可以重塑而非仅仅退化量子操控，建立了脉冲动力学去耦的新范式，这些动力学预计对广泛的量子系统具有普遍意义。

Abstract: One of the defining features of pulsed dynamical decoupling is its suppression of a driven qubit's sensitivity to static detuning errors between the drive field and qubit resonance. In this paper, we show that dissipation, in the form of excited-state decay, can change this behavior entirely, producing an interference signal with a strong detuning dependence. This signal arises from decay during driven evolution and relies on coherence retained by the qubit after such a decay event. We develop analytical and numerical models that capture the underlying mechanism and observe this same dissipation-induced detuning dependence experimentally in both a free-space strontium atom interferometer and a superconducting transmon qubit system. We also use this dissipation-induced detuning dependence as the basis for a new spectroscopic technique called Dissipative Carr-Purcell Spectroscopy (DCPS) and compare it with a traditional Ramsey sequence. Our results establish a regime of pulsed dynamical decoupling in which dissipation reshapes, rather than merely degrades, coherent control, and we expect these dynamics to be relevant to a wide range of quantum systems.

</details>


### [124] [Co-transmission of classical data and continuous-variable entanglement over a single quantum channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25179)
*Nicholas Zaunders,Timothy C. Ralph*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将经典调制量子通信(CMQC)协议应用于位移基同步量子-经典通信(SQCC)协议，解决了SQCC与纠缠分发、中继量子密钥分发等实用量子通信方案不兼容的问题，实现了连续变量高斯纠缠与经典信息的同时分发，并分析了点对点情况下的秘密密钥生成速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始位移基SQCC协议与大多数实用量子通信方案（如纠缠分发、中继QKD）不兼容，因为直接测量经典信号会同时测量并破坏量子态，仅支持点对点高斯QKD。需要解决这一限制，使SQCC能够兼容更广泛的量子通信应用。

Method: 应用Zaunders和Ralph提出的经典调制量子通信(CMQC)协议，以规避SQCC的固有兼容性问题，实现连续变量高斯纠缠与经典信息的同时分发。

Result: 成功演示了连续变量高斯纠缠与经典信息的同时分发；表征了分发纠缠态的质量；概述了该方案适用于中继网络；计算了点对点情况下的秘密密钥生成速率并与等效SQCC协议进行了比较。

Conclusion: CMQC协议能够有效解决SQCC与实用量子通信方案不兼容的问题，使高斯纠缠分发与经典通信可同时进行，且适用于中继网络架构，在点对点场景下具有可比较的密钥生成性能。

Abstract: Displacement-based simultaneous quantum-classical communications (SQCC) protocols, as originally proposed, are generally incompatible with the majority of useful quantum communication schemes, such as entanglement distribution or repeater-based quantum key distribution: direct measurement of the classical signal also measures and destroys the quantum state, leaving point-to-point Gaussian quantum key distribution as the only quantum communication scheme amenable to integration with SQCC. In this work, we apply the classically-modulated quantum communication protocol proposed by Zaunders and Ralph [arXiv:2606.03181v3] to circumvent this issue and demonstrate the distribution of continuous-variable Gaussian entanglement simultaneously with classical information. We characterise the quality of the distributed entangled state and outline how the scheme is suitable for use in repeater-based networks. Lastly, we compute the secret key generation rate of the Gaussian CMQC scheme in the point-to-point case and compare it to the equivalent point-to-point SQCC protocol.

</details>


### [125] [Loss-induced anomalous generalized bunching in multiphoton interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25306)
*Rikizo Ikuta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现被动线性光路中，内部损耗与存活条件性能够激活反常的广义聚束效应。通过引入条件聚束概率（所有光子存活且全部进入目标输出模式区域），证明双光子输入下该概率始终单调，但多模目标区域可反转单调方向；三光子输入在最小三模有损干涉仪中呈现非单调反常，部分可区分光子时条件聚束概率最大，而对应的无条件概率禁止此行为，表明存活条件性损耗改变了广义聚束的最小层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索内部损耗和生存条件性对被动线性光路中广义聚束效应的影响，以及光子可区分性在该过程中的作用。

Method: 引入条件聚束概率（所有光子存活且全部进入目标输出模式区域），通过理论证明和数值计算分析双光子与三光子输入下的聚束行为。

Result: 双光子输入下条件聚束概率始终单调，但多模目标区域可反转单调方向；三光子输入在最小三模有损干涉仪中出现非单调反常，部分可区分光子最大化条件聚束概率。

Conclusion: 存活条件性损耗改变了广义聚束的最小层次结构，使得部分可区分光子能够最大化条件聚束概率，这一行为在无条件概率中被禁止。

Abstract: We show that internal loss and survival conditioning can activate anomalous generalized bunching in passive linear optical circuits. We introduce a conditional bunching probability that all photons occupy a target region of accessible output modes, given that all photons survive. For two-photon inputs, we prove that this probability is always monotonic for any circuit size and loss configuration, although a multimode target region can reverse the monotonic direction. For three-photon inputs in a minimal three-mode lossy interferometer, we find a nonmonotonic anomaly in which the conditional bunching probability is maximized for partially distinguishable photons. This behavior is forbidden for the corresponding unconditioned target-region probability, demonstrating that survival-conditioned loss changes the minimal hierarchy of generalized bunching.

</details>


### [126] [Actualization, Records, and the Emergence of Entropic Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25307)
*Lionel Martellini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子力学中基于记录的内部时间理论，将稳定记录的形成表示为对实现信息的条件作用，每个结果的实际化贡献一个等于其惊喜度（负对数概率）的内部持续时间，其系综平均为香农熵，并阐明了多时钟问题中可加性与Born分布分解的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子力学中的内部时间建立基于记录的严格数学描述，解决时间在量子理论中的内在定义问题，并澄清多时钟情景下的可加性条件。

Method: 通过公理化方法定义内部时间：要求持续时间仅依赖于条件Born概率、在序贯条件下可加、连续且校准，从而推导出持续时间必须等于惊喜度（surprisal）；利用信息论工具（香农熵、Rényi熵谱、Doob分解）刻画时钟的统计性质；通过代数包含序和条件作用形式化记录的形成与更新。

Result: 确定了每个结果的实际化贡献的内部持续时间等于其惊喜度；证明了系综平均时钟为记录过程的香农熵，矩母函数由Rényi熵谱决定；给出了时钟的Doob分解（可预测熵补偿器+鞅波动项）；阐明了多时钟问题：固定上下文下两个惊喜度时钟的可加性等价于Born分布的因子分解；纯二分态下所有局部上下文对的可加性等价于联合态的秩一因子分解及所有2×2子式为零。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子力学中的内禀时间提供了基于信息论和记录理论的严格框架，将时间流逝与信息获取定量联系起来，并澄清了多时钟系统的相容性条件。

Abstract: We develop a record-based account of internal time in quantum mechanics, where the formation of a stable record is represented as conditioning on actualized information, and along a history the accumulated record algebras are ordered by inclusion. If the duration of a realized outcome depends only on its conditional Born probability, composes additively under sequential conditioning, and is continuous and calibrated, then the actualization of each outcome contributes an internal duration equal to its surprisal, the negative logarithm of that probability, so that a certain outcome contributes no duration, whereas less likely outcomes contribute larger increments. The ensemble mean of the accumulated clock is the Shannon entropy of the record process, its moment-generating function is fixed by the Rényi entropy spectrum, and the realized clock admits a Doob decomposition into a predictable entropic compensator and a martingale of clock fluctuations, so that each increment is the information gain of the corresponding actualization. Records are characterized by graded criteria of distinguishability, decoherence, and stability. We also clarify the multiple-clock problem: in one fixed context, additivity of two surprisal clocks is equivalent to factorization of the Born distribution in that context, whereas for a pure bipartite state, additivity in every pair of local contexts is equivalent to rank-one factorization of the joint state and to the vanishing of all its $2\times2$ minors.

</details>


### [127] [Entanglement-based quantum key distribution with data in hollow-core fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25331)
*Yue Luo,Sheng Liu,Yun-Ru Fan,Da-Wei Ge,Zhi-Yang Liu,Hao Li,Si Shen,Zi-Chang Zhang,Hai-Zhi Song,Li-Xing You,Tao Zhou,Kai Guo,Guang-Can Guo,Qiang Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在18公里空芯光纤上实现了量子密钥与经典数据信号的共存，展示了基于纠缠的高维量子密钥分发，在0 dBm双向接收功率下理论数据容量达2.3 Tbps，连续运行24小时平均密钥率10.56 kbps，理论预测在200公里以上距离密钥率可超135 kbps。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纯硅芯光纤因高非线性产生的拉曼散射和四波混频噪声限制了量子信号与经典信号的共存性能，需要一种低非线性噪声的光纤方案以实现可扩展的量子-经典共存网络。

Method: 采用空芯光纤引导光主要在空气中传输，利用其超低非线性特性抑制噪声；在18公里HCF链路上实现基于纠缠的时间编码高维量子密钥分发，同时传输双向经典数据信号。

Result: 实现了0 dBm双向接收功率下理论数据容量2.3 Tbps；连续24小时运行平均密钥率10.56 kbps；理论预测采用低损耗HCF时密钥率在200公里以上超135 kbps。

Conclusion: 空芯光纤相比纯硅芯光纤在量子-经典信号共存方面表现出显著性能提升，展示了HCF在兼容现有光纤网络架构的可扩展量子-经典共存应用中的潜力。

Abstract: The coexistence of quantum information and classical signals in a single fiber is essential for future quantum networks that leverage the well-established optical fiber infrastructure. Although multiplexing technologies can separate quantum and classical signals, pure silica core fibers (PSCFs) remain fundamentally limited by the high nonlinearity, which generates substantial Raman scattering and four-wave mixing noise. Hollow-core fibers (HCFs), guiding light predominantly in air, offer an attractive solution with intrinsically ultra-low nonlinearity and strongly suppressed nonlinear noise. In this work, we demonstrate the entanglement-based key coexisting with data over an 18-km HCF link. We achieve time-encoded high-dimensional quantum key distribution (HD-QKD) carrying 0 dBm of bidirectional received power, corresponding to a theoretical data capacity of up to 2.3 Tbps. During 24 hours of continuous operation, an average secret key rate (SKR) of 10.56 kbps is obtained. Theoretical analysis further predicts SKRs above 135 kbps over transmission distances exceeding 200 km using state-of-the-art low-loss HCFs. These results show significantly improved performance compared with PSCF-based systems and highlight the potential of HCFs for scalable quantum-classical coexistence compatible with the architectures of established fiber-optic networks.

</details>


### [128] [Remote entanglement of massive oscillators via wire-mediated Coulomb interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25341)
*Lorenzo Papa,Onur Hosten,Carlos Gonzalez-Ballestero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出通过在带电宏观机械振子附近放置导体结构来增强库仑相互作用，利用镜像电荷效应改变静电相互作用的距离依赖关系，使耦合从自由空间的1/D³标度变为1/(D ln²D)，从而在更大距离上实现稳态纠缠，理论上可将纠缠距离提升一个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在增强带电宏观机械振子间的库仑相互作用，克服自由空间中静电耦合随距离快速衰减（1/D³）的限制，从而在更远距离上实现宏观振子的量子纠缠，探索基于基本中心力的纠缠生成机制。

Method: 采用宏观量子电动力学推导两个振子的有效运动动力学，通过分析导体（特别是圆柱导线）附近感生镜像电荷对静电相互作用的修正，计算耦合强度的距离依赖关系，并结合连续位置测量分析稳态纠缠的生成条件。

Result: 预言振子间相干运动耦合从自由空间的1/D³标度变为渐近的1/(D ln²D)依赖关系，且对低频振子仅产生可忽略的额外退相干。结合连续位置测量，可在显著更大距离上产生稳态运动纠缠：毫克级振子在数百微米距离上实现可观测纠缠（比自由空间提升一个数量级以上），未来系统改进可达近两个数量级。

Conclusion: 导体辅助库仑相互作用是实现宏观物体量子控制和探索基本中心力产生纠缠的有效资源，为远距离宏观量子纠缠实验提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We propose a method to enhance Coulomb interaction between charged macroscopic mechanical oscillators by placing a conducting structure in their vicinity. We derive the effective motional dynamics of the two oscillators using macroscopic quantum electrodynamics and show that image charges induced in the conductor fundamentally modify the range of the electrostatic interaction. For the specific case of a cylindrical wire, we predict that the coherent motional coupling changes from the free-space scaling $1/D^3$ to an asymptotic $1/(D\ln^2 D)$ dependence on the separation $D$ between the oscillators, at the cost of only negligible additional decoherence for low-frequency oscillators. We further show that, when combined with continuous position measurements, the enhanced interaction enables the generation of steady-state motional entanglement between the oscillators over significantly larger distances than achievable in free space. For experimentally realistic milligram-scale oscillators, we predict observable entanglement at separations of several hundred microns -- more than an order of magnitude beyond free-space capabilities -- with improvements approaching two orders of magnitude in future systems. These results identify conductor-assisted Coulomb interactions as a resource for quantum control of massive objects and for the exploration of entanglement generated by fundamental central forces.

</details>


### [129] [Quantum teleportation over a field-deployed hollow-core fibre network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25352)
*Ri-Yao Song,Ya-Zhou Zhao,Yun-Ru Fan,Yang-Bin Ma,Yan-Yu Wei,Si Shen,Hao Li,Li-Xing You,Kai Guo,Guang-Can Guo,Qiang Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究在成都城域部署的空芯光纤网络中，实现了与经典数据流量共存的量子隐形传态实验。空芯光纤凭借其极低的拉曼噪声（比标准实芯光纤低约三个数量级）和无需主动稳定的长期光子不可区分性，在经典发射功率高达160 mW的条件下仍能实现鲁棒的量子传态，为可扩展量子网络的经典-量子兼容架构提供了波长无关、即插即用的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子隐形传态在实际部署中受到量子信道损伤（如损耗、噪声、波动）的严重影响，尤其在经典数据流量或背景光共存的场景下性能严重退化。实现可扩展量子网络的关键在于开发能够同时支持高保真量子操作和高容量经典通信的先进信道架构。

Method: 利用成都城域部署的三节点空芯光纤网络，在共传播经典流量条件下实现中间贝尔态测量的量子隐形传态。空芯光纤结合了自由空间般的弱光-物质相互作用与光纤系统的稳定性，无需主动稳定即可保持光量子比特的长期不可区分性。

Result: 空芯光纤链路无需主动稳定即可保持光量子比特的长期不可区分性；其拉曼噪声比标准实芯光纤低约三个数量级；在经典发射功率高达160 mW时仍能实现鲁棒的量子隐形传态。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了在已部署光纤基础设施上实现经典数据兼容的量子网络框架，为量子互联网提供了一条波长无关、即插即用、无需主动稳定的实现路径。

Abstract: When a photon and one member of an entangled photon pair are jointly projected onto a Bell-state measurement (BSM), the quantum state of the photon can be transferred to the distant partner of the pair without physically transmitting this information carrier. In real-world deployment, however, teleportation performance is fundamentally bottlenecked by quantum channel impairments, such as loss, noise, and fluctuations, which induce severe decoherence and degrade fidelity. This vulnerability is further exacerbated in scenarios with intense classical data traffic or background light. Realizing scalable quantum networks, therefore, hinges on developing advanced channel architectures capable of supporting both high-fidelity quantum operations and high-capacity classical communications within a shared infrastructure. Towards this end, hollow core fibre (HCF) offers a promising quantum channel resource by combining free-space-like weak light-matter interaction with the stability of fibre-based systems. Here, utilizing a field-deployed metropolitan HCF network spanning three spatially separated nodes in Chengdu, we achieve quantum teleportation with an intermediate BSM under co-propagating classical traffic. Crucially, the HCF links preserve the long-term indistinguishability of photonic qubits without active stabilization, and exhibit a Raman noise approximately three orders of magnitude lower than that of standard solid-core counterparts. This noise suppression enables robust quantum teleportation even alongside classical launch powers up to 160 mW. Our findings establish a classical-data-compatible framework for quantum networking over deployed fibre infrastructure and offer a wavelength-agnostic, plug-and-play, and free-running pathway toward the quantum internet.

</details>


### [130] [Quantum Teleportation toward the Quantum Internet: A Concise Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25395)
*Yang-Bin Ma,Yun-Ru Fan,Ri-Yao Song,Ya-Zhou Zhao,Yi-Ye Liu,Si Shen,Zi-Hao Zhan,Yan-Yu Wei,Kai Guo,Guang-Can Guo,Qiang Zhou*

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于量子隐形传态构建量子网络的关键实验进展，讨论了实现可扩展实用化量子互联网面临的挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络是量子信息科学的核心基础设施，而量子隐形传态是长距离传输量子态、分配量子资源的关键技术。本文旨在综述不同物理平台上基于量子隐形传态构建量子网络的代表性实验，为最终实现全球尺度量子互联网提供系统性参考。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统梳理了跨不同物理平台（如光子、固态、原子等）的量子隐形传态实验，并分析了量子中继协议在实现任意节点间量子通信中的作用。

Result: 综述呈现了多个物理平台上量子隐形传态的实验实现，展示了量子网络构建的可行路径；同时指出了当前技术瓶颈，如退相干、纠缠分发距离限制、中继效率等。

Conclusion: 量子隐形传态与量子中继协议是实现全球量子互联网的可行途径，但距离大规模实用化仍面临诸多挑战，需要跨平台协同发展与技术突破。

Abstract: Quantum networks play a pivotal role in quantum information science, which not only provide a secure communication platform for remote access to quantum computers but also serve as the strategic core for achieving large-scale quantum information processing, forming the foundational infrastructure for the future global-scale quantum internet. Quantum teleportation, which enables the transmission of unknown quantum states over long distances by employing quantum entanglement together with classical communication, is essential for the distribution of quantum resources in the construction of the global-scale quantum internet. To realize a global-scale quantum internet, quantum repeater protocols represent one of the most promising approaches for enabling quantum communication between any nodes. This concise review presents representative experimental demonstrations of quantum teleportation for constructing quantum networks across different physical platforms. Along this trajectory, the review discusses current challenges, open issues, and future perspectives toward scalable and practical quantum internet.

</details>


### [131] [A General First- and Second-Order Numerical Solver for Non-Markovian Quantum State Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25427)
*Zhenning Cai,Quanhui Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对非马尔可夫开放量子系统的数值模拟，提出了一种基于非马尔可夫量子态扩散（NMQSD）方程的通用辅助态框架，通过解析求解线性NMQSD方程揭示三种基本结构，并构建了无需预分解浴关联函数的一阶和二阶数值格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非马尔可夫开放量子系统数值方法通常依赖于浴关联函数的特定分解形式，缺乏能够直接处理任意浴关联函数中泛函导数的通用数值框架。

Method: 首先解析求解线性NMQSD方程，揭示随机传播、泛函导数插入和记忆配对三种基本结构；然后基于此结构构建通用辅助态框架，将数值构造分解为时间离散化、记忆求积和层次截断三个步骤；最后构建一阶和二阶数值格式并提供图解转移规则。

Result: 数值结果验证了所提方法具有预期的时间精度，并且能够适用于不同的浴关联函数和多能级量子系统。

Conclusion: 该工作为非马尔可夫开放量子系统的数值模拟提供了一种通用的、无需特定浴关联函数分解的辅助态框架，具有良好的精度和广泛的适用性。

Abstract: The numerical simulation of non-Markovian open quantum systems based on the non-Markovian quantum state diffusion (NMQSD) equation is complicated by functional derivatives with respect to the stochastic process. A general numerical framework that directly treats these functional derivatives without relying on prescribed decompositions of the bath correlation function is still lacking. In this work, we derive an analytical solution of the linear NMQSD equation that reveals three elementary structures of the non-Markovian stochastic dynamics: stochastic propagation, functional-derivative insertion, and memory pairing. Based on this structure, we construct a general auxiliary-state framework for arbitrary bath correlation functions. The framework separates the numerical construction into time discretization, memory quadrature, and hierarchy truncation. We then construct first- and second-order schemes and provide diagrammatic transition rules for their explicit implementation. Numerical results verify the expected temporal accuracy and demonstrate the applicability of the proposed methods to different bath correlation functions and multi-level quantum systems.

</details>


### [132] [Asymmetric information scrambling and eigenstate thermalization in inhomogeneous XXZ spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25496)
*Shivam Mishra,Ravi Prakash*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非均匀XXZ自旋链中确定性空间相互作用梯度对离时序关联子（OTOC）的影响，发现有限相互作用梯度（δ>0）会导致信息加扰的左右不对称性，并通过ETH框架和解析表达式解释了长时饱和值不对称的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性空间非均匀性在实验设计的量子多体系统中日益重要，相互作用梯度会强烈影响非平衡动力学，但目前对其在信息加扰中的作用理解尚不充分。

Method: 采用确定性空间变化的相互作用剖面，研究非均匀XXZ自旋链中的OTOC及其与ETH的联系。通过两种互补方法分析OTOC有限尺寸长时饱和值的不对称性：一是ETH框架下分析OTOC可观测量在能量本征基下的对角矩阵元；二是推导基于哈密顿量与OTOC可观测量重叠的解析表达式，明确包含空间相互作用剖面。

Result: 有限相互作用梯度（δ>0）导致OTOC量化的信息加扰出现明显的左右不对称性；即使系统呈现量子混沌的谱特征，强相互作用侧算符的加扰仍被抑制；解析预测与数值结果完全一致。

Conclusion: 确定性相互作用梯度能够产生OTOC长时饱和值的观测不对称性，解析表达式提供了其微观解释，揭示了空间非均匀性如何影响量子多体系统的信息加扰行为。

Abstract: Deterministic spatial inhomogeneity has become increasingly relevant in experimentally engineered quantum many-body systems, where interaction gradients can strongly influence nonequilibrium dynamics. Motivated by this, we investigate out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs) and their connection to the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH) in inhomogeneous XXZ spin chains. Using a deterministic spatially varying interaction profile, we show that finite interaction gradients ($δ>0$) induce a pronounced left--right asymmetry in information scrambling, as quantified by OTOCs. This asymmetry persists even when the system exhibits spectral signatures of quantum chaos, with operators on the strongly interacting side exhibiting suppressed scrambling. To elucidate the origin of the asymmetric finite-size long-time saturation value of OTOCs, we employ two complementary approaches. First, we analyze the diagonal matrix elements of the OTOC observables in the energy eigenbasis within the ETH framework. Second, we derive an analytical expression for the finite-size saturation value based on the overlap between the Hamiltonian and the OTOC observables, which explicitly incorporates the spatial interaction profile. The analytical prediction is fully consistent with the numerical results and provides a microscopic explanation for how deterministic interaction gradients generate the observed asymmetry in the long-time saturation of OTOCs.

</details>

*Marcello Armezzani,Colin P. Lualdi,Xuemei Gu,Paul G. Kwiat,Mario Krenn*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子之间弱相互作用使得确定性组装大规模多光子纠缠态成为量子光学中的核心挑战，亟需新的理论方案来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 使用AI技术自动发现 heralded 线性光学方案，并从发现的单个电路方案中提取可转移的物理理解，推广为通用的可扩展方案族。

Result: 发现的新方案族在包含已知构造作为特例的同时，提供了指数级和超指数级的性能改进，并可扩展到更广泛的目标纠缠态类别。

Conclusion: 该工作为光子 heralding 提供了理论进展，并为实验上实现大规模多光子纠缠态提供了切实可行的路径，有望推动光量子技术在多方面的实际应用。

Abstract: Entangled states of light lie at the heart of photonic quantum technologies, from distributed quantum communication to quantum-enhanced measurement and information processing. Their practical generation, however, remains constrained by the weak interactions between photons, which make the deterministic assembly of large multiphoton entangled states a central challenge in quantum optics. In this work, we use AI techniques to discover heralded linear-optical schemes for path-entangled states and show that the resulting solutions can be elevated from individual circuits to a new scalable family. This family contains previously known constructions as special cases while generally providing exponential and super-exponential improvements over those, and its extension to broader classes of target states shows how automated discovery can reveal transferable physical understanding. By presenting compact experimental proposals for large path-entangled states, our results provide both a theoretical advance in photonic heralding and a route towards a substantial leap in experimentally accessible multiphoton entanglement.

</details>
