TL;DR: 本文研究了多能级量子发射体在集体光物质态中的影响，发现当发射体间距较近时，近共振跃迁间的干涉会完全改变集体态特性，并存在超辐射发生的间距下限；间距较大时则与二能级近似结果相似。提出可用光学晶格中的分子实现这两种 regime 并给出实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究广泛采用二能级近似处理量子发射体，忽略了实际发射体的多能级结构。本文旨在揭示多能级效应对集体光物质态的影响，并探索其与二能级近似下的差异。

Method: 理论分析多能级量子发射体的集体态特性，对比二能级近似情形，研究近共振跃迁干涉对超辐射条件的影响，并以光学晶格中的分子为候选体系进行理论建模。

Result: 发现了多能级发射体超辐射发生的间距下限（二能级近似中不存在）；近间距时多能级干涉效应显著改变集体态；大间距时多能级结果趋近于二能级近似。

Conclusion: 多能级效应在紧密排列的发射体中不可忽略，提出了可实验实现该效应的分子候选体系及可观测的实验特征。

Abstract: We explore how collective states of light and matter differ when the multi-level nature of the quantum emitters is fully taken into account. For closely spaced emitters, interferences between near-resonant transitions completely change the character of the collective states compared to two-level approximations. In particular, we find a lower bound on the emitter separation for superradiance to occur which does not exist for two-level emitters. By contrast, for larger separations between the emitters, the collective states resemble those obtained within the widely used two-level approximation of the emitters. Both regimes may be realized by molecules trapped in optical lattices. We therefore propose molecular candidates and describe experimental signatures of emerging multi-level interference.

</details>


### [135] [Theory of Cubic-Phase Dynamics in the Linear Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25627)
*Maximilian L. D. D. Pellner,Georgi Gary Rozenman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究了线性势（如重力）中量子波包的立方时间相位，揭示了其由本征力与施加力共同决定的解析形式，并通过Airy本征态实现无弥散传播，最后在超冷原子、近轴光学和水波三个平台上验证了相对相位信号的提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性势中波包积累的立方时间相位具有普适性，但以往研究对其内部结构、力的相互作用机制及其可观测性缺乏系统解析，作者希望建立完整的理论框架并探索其实验可测方案。

Method: 基于Airy本征态进行无量纲化，引入本征力概念；通过解析推导得到立方相位系数的闭合表达式；采用外差解调技术从两个碰撞Airy波包的干涉信号中提取相对相位。

Result: 立方相位系数具有因子化形式，沿两条零线（静态Airy本征态和非平凡零点）为零；本征力与施加力构成对抗关系但不影响质心；同源波包因对称性禁止产生相对相位，制备不对称可激活可设计信号；模拟干涉外差解调结果与理论预测在亚百分比精度内吻合。

Conclusion: 线性势中波包的立方相位包含三种贡献且可解析求解，本征力作为自然参数揭示了相位抵消的非平凡机制，为跨平台（超冷原子、光学、水波）的量子相位测量提供了可设计信号方案。

Abstract: A quantum wave packet in a linear potential, i.e., under a constant force such as gravity, accumulates a cubic-in-time phase that is universal across Schrodinger-type platforms and naturally realized by Airy eigenstates. Because the classical action is quadratic in the force, this phase comprises exactly three contributions: intrinsic, force-induced, and a cross term. The force-induced contribution alone is shape-independent, whereas the Airy eigenstate renders the shape-dependent contributions non-dispersing. An eigenstate-based nondimensionalization identifies the eigenforce, namely the intrinsic force underlying the packet's acceleration in the absence of an applied force, as a natural parameter. As a function of both forces, the cubic coefficient takes an analytically closed and physically interpretable form that factors along two zero lines: the static Airy eigenstate and a nontrivial zero at which the phase cancels without stationarity. This exposes the eigenforce as an effective antagonist to the applied force, not only in the caustic's self-acceleration but also within the phase, while leaving the centroid unaffected in accordance with Ehrenfest's theorem. Spatially uniform within each packet, the phase cannot be measured directly and is accessible only through the relative phase of two colliding packets, each evolving in its own potential. The general relative cubic coefficient, forbidden by symmetry for identical packets and activated by preparation asymmetry, therefore provides a designable signal. Extracted through heterodyne demodulation of the simulated interference between two Airy packets, its central value agrees with the prediction to sub-percent accuracy within the fitting uncertainty. The analysis spans ultracold-atom condensates, paraxial optics, and surface-gravity water waves.

</details>


### [136] [Coherent control of subradiant excitations in atomic rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25638)
*Milena Djatchkova,Igor Lesanovsky,Beatriz Olmos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在环形原子阵列中理论证明了亚辐射激子的局域捕获、绝热输运、环间相干转移以及相互作用诱导的条件相位移动，展示了有序原子阵列作为耗散保护集体激发实现光子量子信息处理的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚辐射态因辐射衰减极低，是存储和操控光子激发的理想平台，但如何实现激发的局域捕获和相干操控仍是关键问题。

Method: 在环形原子阵列中，通过外部几何结构结合原子偶极取向的动态控制，实现亚辐射激发的局域捕获；利用绝热输运实现单环内激发的移动；通过几何控制的选通性实现环间相干转移；利用相互作用诱导的条件相位移动实现受控相位门操作。

Result: 成功展示了单环内激发绝热输运、双环间几何选通性相干转移，以及相邻环中两个同时被捕获的激发之间的条件相位移动，后者可解释为存储激发之间的有效受控相位操作。

Conclusion: 有序原子阵列可作为耗散保护集体激发实现相干光子量子信息处理的平台。

Abstract: Collective excitations in ordered subwavelength atomic arrays can exhibit strongly suppressed radiative decay due to interference between light scattered by neighboring emitters. These so-called subradiant states make these systems a promising platform for storing and manipulating photonic excitations. The external geometry of the array, combined with dynamical control of the atomic dipole orientation, enables localized trapping and coherent transport of these subradiant excitations. Here, we theoretically demonstrate these capabilities in ring-shaped atomic arrays. Specifically, we show adiabatic transport of a localized excitation around a single ring, coherent transfer of a single excitation between two neighboring rings with geometry-controlled selectivity, and interaction-induced conditional phase shifts between two simultaneously trapped excitations in neighboring rings. The latter can be interpreted as effective controlled-phase operations between stored excitations. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of ordered atomic arrays as a platform for coherent photonic quantum information processing with dissipation-protected collective excitations.

</details>


### [137] [Nonlocal Magnonic Cat States in Hybrid Magnon-Qubit Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25643)
*Urmimala Dewan,Roson Nongthombam,Sampreet Kalita,Amarendra K. Sarma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于纠缠交换的协议，通过在超导transmon量子比特与钇铁石榴石(YIG)球体组成的混合架构中执行投影贝尔态测量，在空间分离的子系统间生成非局域的双粒子magnonic猫态，并利用Wigner函数重构进行实验表征，为长距离magnonic纠缠和混合量子网络架构提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了建立玻色子态（如猫态）的长距离量子网络，实现可扩展量子通信与分布式量子计算，需要将基于相干态量子叠加的玻色编码从本地信息处理扩展到远程纠缠分发。

Method: 采用纠缠交换协议：每个子系统由超导transmon量子比特与YIG球体（支持磁振子模式）耦合构成；先建立局域磁振子-量子比特纠缠，再对量子比特进行投影贝尔态测量，将纠缠相干转移到远程磁振子模式，生成非局域magnonic猫态；通过联合位移宇称测量重构Wigner函数进行量子态层析表征。

Result: 成功在空间分离的子系统间生成了双粒子magnonic猫态，并通过Wigner函数重构实现了量子态层析表征。

Conclusion: 该方案为实现长距离magnonic纠缠提供了可行路径，推动了混合量子网络架构的发展。

Abstract: The quantum superpositions of coherent states offer an alternative to the conventional qubit-based encodings by harnessing the large Hilbert space available in bosonic modes, including those realised in microwave and optical cavities, magnons, and mechanical resonators. Beyond their advantages for local information processing, establishing long-distance quantum networks for such bosonic states is crucial for scalable quantum communication and distributed quantum computation. In this work, we propose an entanglement-swapping-based protocol to generate a bipartite magnonic cat state shared between spatially separated subsystems. Each subsystem comprises a hybrid architecture consisting of a superconducting transmon qubit coupled to a yttrium iron garnet (YIG) sphere that supports magnon modes. By performing a projective Bell-state measurement on the qubits, the initially established magnon-qubit entanglement is coherently transferred to the remote magnon modes, resulting in a nonlocal magnonic cat state. For experimental characterisation of the gener- ated states, we perform quantum state tomography through reconstruction of the Wigner function using joint displaced parity measurements of the magnon modes. Our scheme provides a feasible route towards realising long-distance magnonic entanglement and contributes to the advancement of hybrid quantum network architectures.

</details>


### [138] [Engineering a Quantum Thermal Diode with Floquet Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25678)
*Aqsa Rehman,M. Tahir Naseem,Adam Zaman Chaudhry*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Floquet调控机制的量子热二极管，通过对两个Ising耦合的量子比特施加纵向调制，实现了热流整流。单侧驱动可打破对称性，实现单向热流阻断而另一方向保持传输；双侧驱动则需同时阻断Floquet泵浦和相互作用通道。该机制为最小量子热器件中可控热流不对称性提供了设计原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态热二极管常面临整流效果与热流传输之间的折衷（tradeoff），即在抑制反向热流的同时也会显著降低正向热流。本文旨在通过Floquet驱动机制解决这一矛盾，实现强整流而不牺牲传输热流。

Method: 建立由两个纵向量子调制、Ising耦合的量子比特组成的量子热二极管模型，每个比特耦合至独立热库。从微观系统-浴模型出发，推导Floquet-LGKS主方程，解析驱动辅助的跃迁通道。分析单侧驱动和双侧驱动两种配置下的稳态热流。

Result: 单侧驱动下获得紧凑的稳态热流公式和精确的阻断条件；弱正弦驱动下整流效应随调制振幅二次增长。双侧驱动需同时抵消Floquet泵浦贡献和相互作用贡献才能实现完全阻断。

Conclusion: 接触选择性Floquet修饰（contact-selective Floquet dressing）可作为可控热流不对称性的设计原理，为量子热二极管提供新范式。

Abstract: Controlling heat flow in small quantum systems is a central goal of quantum thermodynamics and nanoscale transport. A key challenge is to achieve strong thermal rectification without suppressing the transmitted heat current, a tradeoff that often arises in static diode configurations. We establish a Floquet-control mechanism in a minimal quantum thermal diode formed by two longitudinally modulated Ising-coupled qubits, each coupled to an independent thermal reservoir. From a microscopic system--bath model, we derive a Floquet--LGKS master equation that resolves the drive-assisted transition channels. The resonant undriven device with left--right symmetric bath couplings serves as the reciprocal benchmark, while static detuning provides a rectifying reference with reduced heat current. Single-side driving breaks this reciprocal structure by creating a Floquet-dressed contact opposite a purely thermal contact. For this configuration, we obtain a compact steady-state current formula and an exact blocking condition for suppressing one bias direction while retaining finite transport in the opposite direction. In the weak sinusoidal-drive regime, rectification begins quadratically in the modulation amplitude. Dual-side driving adds a Floquet pumping contribution to the interaction-mediated current, so complete blocking requires cancellation of both contributions. These results establish contact-selective Floquet dressing as a design principle for controllable heat-flow asymmetry in minimal quantum thermal devices.

</details>


### [139] [Counterfactual Quantum Sensing: What Interaction-Free Measurement Can and Cannot Buy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25699)
*Christoph F. Wildfeuer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从估计理论角度分析了相互作用自由测量的实际价值。使用Elitzur-Vaidman干涉仪进行通道估计时，Fisher信息量仅为直接透射探测的一半，但每吸收一个光子的Fisher信息量相同。优势在于区分而非估计：区分物体存在与不存在时，Chernoff信息随Zeno循环次数几乎线性增长；但区分两个部分透明度时则无增长。寄生损耗会限制这一优势。对于任意固定光学系统，多程探测的可达量子Fisher信息不会超过相同入射通量的独立单程探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究相互作用自由测量（如Elitzur-Vaidman干涉仪）在估计理论意义上的实际优势，特别是对比直接透射探测的性能差异，以及理解其真正有价值的应用场景。

Method: 将Elitzur-Vaidman干涉仪建模为通道估计问题，比较其与直接透射探测的Fisher信息量；采用Chernoff信息分析区分不同假设时的性能；考虑寄生损耗对性能的影响；推导一般多程探测的量子Fisher信息上界。

Result: 干涉仪的Fisher信息是直接探测的一半；每吸收一个光子的Fisher信息两者相同；区分存在与不存在时Chernoff信息随Zeno周期数几乎线性增长；区分两个部分透明度时无增长；寄生损耗导致可达优势受限于损耗每周期倒数；任意固定光学系统的多程探测不会超过相同通量的单程探测。

Conclusion: 相互作用自由测量在估计透明度方面无优势，其价值在于判别物体是否存在而非测量透明度。损耗每周期是决定性能的关键品质因数。Massar-Mitchison-Pironio界可由此简洁推导。

Abstract: Interaction-free measurement infers the presence of an absorbing object from a photon that, in the counterfactual sense, never interacted with it, and is widely described as a route to minimally invasive sensing. We ask what it actually buys, in estimation-theoretic terms. Written as a channel-estimation problem, the Elitzur-Vaidman interferometer carries exactly half the Fisher information about the object's transmissivity that direct transmission probing does, and the two schemes deliver identical Fisher information per absorbed photon. For measuring how transparent something is, the interferometer buys nothing. The advantage lies in discrimination, and we show that what it requires is not that the object be opaque but that the competing hypothesis be the object's absence. Against empty space the Chernoff information per absorbed photon grows almost linearly, as the number of Zeno cycles times its logarithm, even for a weakly absorbing object; between two partial transparencies it does not grow at all. Parasitic loss in the cycle caps the advantage. The number of conclusive interrogations per absorbed photon reaches a maximum inversely proportional to the loss per cycle, at an optimal cycle number that is likewise inversely proportional to it, which identifies the loss per cycle as the figure of merit governing how far interaction-free sensing can be pushed. Finally, the negative result is not special to the interferometer. For a single photon meeting a memoryless, non-dispersive object any number of times through arbitrary fixed optics, the accessible quantum Fisher information never exceeds that of the same incident flux spent on independent single-pass probes, and is generically far below it. This recovers the bound of Massar, Mitchison and Pironio for this class, by a short argument that also identifies when it is tight.

</details>


### [140] [Benchmarking Optical Receivers for Quantum Communication and Randomness Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25702)
*Hamid Tebyanian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在部分设备无关框架下比较了连续变量、光子计数和混合接收器在量子随机数生成中的性能，发现连续变量接收器在中低能量下表现最优，而混合接收器在低能量且有信标标签时更具优势；光子计数因相位盲特性无法认证最差情况随机性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光接收器的选择决定了发送态哪些属性在观测数据中可见，进而影响不同量子协议的性能。本文旨在系统比较三类接收器在相同制备-测量框架下的表现。

Method: 采用半设备无关的随机数生成框架，测量设备未表征，源通过Gram矩阵（能量导出重叠约束、幅度Gram基准或完整复Gram矩阵）描述。观测统计量用于约束H_min(B|X,Λ)（针对与测量设备相关但与输入无关的经典侧信息），该界由精确的SDP得到，复多输入情形以块实形式处理并通过对偶证书验证。

Result: 光子计数对固定模相位编码是相位盲的，无法认证最差情况随机性；连续变量接收器在中低能量下给出最高认证熵；混合接收器在低能量且保留信标区域标签时表现更优。

Conclusion: 该接收器统计量框架也可用于离散调制连续变量QKD、量子读取、隐蔽通信和量子签名验证的接收器级别比较，但各协议仍需完整安全性分析。

Abstract: The choice of optical receiver determines which properties of the transmitted states remain visible in the observed data and therefore affects the performance of different quantum protocols. We compare continuous-variable, photon-counting and hybrid receivers within the same prepare-and-measure framework, using semi-device-independent randomness generation as the main case study. The measurement device is left uncharacterised, while the source is described by the Gram matrix of its pure signal states using an energy-derived overlap constraint, a magnitude-Gram benchmark or the full complex Gram matrix of a certified coherent phase-shift-keyed constellation. Within this framework, the observed receiver statistics are used to bound $H_{\min}(B|X,Λ)$ against classical side information correlated with the measurement device but independent of the input. For a fixed Gram matrix, this bound is obtained from an exact semidefinite program, with complex multi-input cases treated in block-real form and checked through the corresponding dual certificate. Photon counting alone is phase blind for fixed-modulus phase encoding and therefore certifies no worst-case randomness. Continuous-variable receivers give the highest certified entropy at moderate energy, while under the nominal source calibration a hybrid receiver performs better at low energy when the beacon-region label is retained in the output. The same receiver statistics also provide receiver-level comparisons for discrete-modulated continuous-variable quantum key distribution, quantum reading, covert communication and quantum-signature verification, without replacing the full security analysis required for each protocol.

</details>


### [141] [Verifiable blind probabilistic error cancellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25704)
*Bo Yang,Elham Kashefi,Harold Ollivier*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了可验证盲概率误差消除(VBPEC)协议，这是首个将量子误差缓解(QEM)与完全恶意敌手下的安全验证相结合的协议。VBPEC在保持无量子空间开销的同时，实现了完美盲化和指数级小的安全误差，通过将陷门验证从确定性通过/失败检查扩展为受益于QEM的统计测试，从根本上提高了量子计算验证的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子误差缓解(QEM)技术虽然能缓解硬件噪声且不增加量子空间开销，但其可靠性依赖于建模、校准和实现，在不可信量子硬件上的端到端安全性问题尚未解决。作者旨在解决这一安全漏洞，将QEM纳入可组合安全框架。

Method: VBPEC将概率误差消除(PEC)纳入抽象密码学框架中的可组合安全范畴，将委托缓解形式化为密码学资源；扩展了基于陷门的验证方法，从确定性通过/失败检查发展为受益于QEM的统计测试；并开发了新的证明技术来整合额外偏差源。

Result: VBPEC实现了完美盲化和指数级小的安全误差，保留了无量子空间开销的特性，唯一开销来自QEM过程引入的额外重复次数。与仅在固定阈值下容忍诚实噪声不同，VBPEC能主动消除噪声，使正确缓解的估计值以高概率被接受而不损害安全性。

Conclusion: VBPEC建立了通往安全、可靠且实用的近未来量子硬件委托量子计算的关键路径，从根本上提高了量子计算验证的实用性。

Abstract: Quantum error mitigation (QEM) is an essential tool for mitigating hardware noise without incurring space overhead. Yet, its reliability depends on modeling, calibration, and implementation, leaving end-to-end security on untrusted quantum hardware unresolved. We address this problem by introducing verifiable blind probabilistic error cancellation (VBPEC), the first secure verification protocol against a fully malicious adversary that integrates QEM. VBPEC brings probabilistic error cancellation (PEC), a widely studied QEM technique, within the scope of composable security by formalizing delegated mitigation as a cryptographic resource in the abstract cryptography framework. The protocol performs PEC with perfect blindness and an exponentially small security error. VBPEC retains the absence of quantum-space overhead from recent statistically-secure verified quantum computation protocols and from PEC. The only overhead takes the form of additional repetitions due to the QEM procedure. To achieve this, we extend trap-based verification from deterministic pass/fail checks to statistical tests that benefit from QEM and develop a new proof technique that integrates the corresponding additional deviation sources. Rather than merely tolerating honest noise below a fixed threshold, VBPEC actively cancels it, enabling correctly mitigated estimates to be accepted with high probability without compromising security. Our framework thus establishes an essential route towards secure, reliable, and practical delegated quantum computation on near-future quantum hardware: VBPEC fundamentally improves the practicality of verification.

</details>


### [142] [Classically Augmented Zero-Noise Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25746)
*Timon Scheiber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合量子-经典方法Classically Augmented Zero-Noise Extrapolation（CA-ZNE），用经典模拟估计替换高噪声Richardson外推节点，在截断偏差足够小时显著降低均方误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零噪声外推（ZNE）方法在高噪声节点处采样方差大，限制了误差缓解效果。

Method: 将高噪声Richardson外推节点替换为经典模拟估计，这些节点具有可忽略的采样方差但引入确定性模拟偏差；推导了最优射击分配下的方差缩减，并分析了线性节点间距和固定指数截断下的系数级指数级缩减。

Result: 理论推导表明在线性节点间距和固定指数截断下可获得系数级的指数级方差缩减；通过Pauli传播数值模拟验证了在截断偏差足够小时均方误差的降低。

Conclusion: CA-ZNE通过混合量子-经典方式有效降低了误差缓解中的采样方差，在偏差可控时优于纯量子ZNE方法。

Abstract: We investigate a hybrid quantum-classical approach to quantum error mitigation. We propose Classically Augmented Zero-Noise Extrapolation, a hybrid error-mitigation method in which high-noise Richardson extrapolation nodes are replaced by classically simulated estimates. These classical nodes have negligible sampling variance but introduce deterministic simulation bias. We derive the resulting variance reduction under optimal shot allocation and show that, for linear node spacings and fixed index cutoff, the coefficient-level reduction can be exponential. We validate the prediction numerically using Pauli-propagation simulations and demonstrate a reduction in mean-squared error when the truncation bias is sufficiently small.

</details>


### [143] [Exponential de Finetti Theorems for Fermionic Gaussian States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25779)
*Jędrzej Burkat,Michał Studziński,Sergii Strelchuk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个指数型高斯de Finetti定理：置换不变的自由费米子高斯态的子系可以被几乎i.i.d.态的凸组合良好逼近，误差随未约束部分数量指数衰减，并在小子系时达到超指数衰减。维数惩罚仅呈多对数增长，较标准de Finetti定理实现了指数级改进。同时将结果从高斯对称态推广到更广的高斯不变态类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息理论中，de Finetti定理是证明量子密钥分发等协议安全性的核心工具。标准de Finetti定理的误差界随局域希尔伯特空间维度呈多项式惩罚，这限制了其在高维量子系统中的应用。作者希望针对自由费米子高斯态这一重要量子态类，建立指数衰减且维度惩罚仅为多对数增长的de Finetti型定理。

Method: 1) 针对置换不变的自由费米子高斯态，构建近似i.i.d.的高斯态的凸组合，推导误差指数衰减的严格上界；2) 引入"高斯不变态"这一更广的态类，证明其与高斯对称态的关系——高斯不变态是局部扩维副本上高斯对称态的部分迹，且总能纯化为更大的高斯对称态；3) 将de Finetti定理从高斯对称态扩展至高斯不变态，仅付出多项式维数惩罚开销。

Result: 误差界随未约束部分数量指数衰减，子系小时更是超指数衰减；维数惩罚为多对数级别，较标准de Finetti定理的指数惩罚实现了指数级改进；完整恢复了[arXiv:2603.12392]中全i.i.d.极限下的高斯de Finetti定理；成功将结果推广至高斯不变态类，仅增加多项式维数惩罚。

Conclusion: 建立了指数型高斯de Finetti定理，在误差衰减速率和维度惩罚两方面均大幅优于标准de Finetti定理；将适用范围从高斯对称态扩展至高斯不变态，并揭示了二者间通过局部扩维和纯化操作的深刻联系；为高维自由费米子系统的量子信息处理提供了更高效的理论工具。

Abstract: We prove an exponential variant of the Gaussian de Finetti theorem: the subsystems of permutation-invariant, free-fermionic Gaussian states are well-approximated by convex combinations of almost-i.i.d. states that are Gaussian on subsets of their parts. Our result provides an error bound between the original state and its approximants that decays exponentially in the number of unconstrained parts, becoming super-exponential when the subsystem under consideration is small. The dimensional penalty of our bound is polylogarithmic in the local Hilbert space dimension, an exponential improvement over the standard de Finetti theorem of [Nat. Phys. 3, 645-649]. In the fully i.i.d. limit, our bound recovers the Gaussian de Finetti theorem of [arXiv:2603.12392]. Previous works considered Gaussian-symmetric states, which are supported on the trivial irrep of the tensor matchgate representation. We extend these to a broader class of Gaussian-invariant states containing, for example, i.i.d. copies of single-replica mixed Gaussian states. We show that Gaussian-invariant states are precisely the partial traces of Gaussian-symmetric states on locally enlarged replicas, and always admit a purification into a larger Gaussian-symmetric state. This extends de Finetti theorems to the full set of Gaussian-invariant states, with only a polynomial overhead in the dimensional penalty of the error bound.

</details>


### [144] [Efficient Lindbladian Learning from Constant-Time Pauli Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25795)
*Jiaxing Song,Yukun Zhang,Xiao Yuan,Yusen Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个高效的林德布拉德学习框架，通过将局部泡利响应视为生成元项的线性系统，解决开放量子系统中相干-耗散歧义问题，并开发了两种学习算法（切比雪夫-洛巴托响应插值和单时间投影响应收缩），能以$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(M/ε^2)$的复杂度实现M个候选系数的逐元素$\epsilon$精度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放多体系统的生成元学习比哈密顿量学习更具挑战性，因为局部响应在包含相干相互作用项的同时也含有耗散贡献，导致相干-耗散歧义问题。现有方法难以从短时数据中有效分离这两类贡献，亟需可扩展的学习框架。

Method: 提出林德布拉德学习框架：1) 将局部泡利响应建模为相干项和耗散项的线性系统；2) 通过反转响应系统分离贡献；3) 开发两种算法——切比雪夫-洛巴托响应插值（对数数量短时演化）和单时间投影响应收缩（单一固定演化时间全局反演截断响应函数）。

Result: 两种算法均能以$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(M/ε^2)$的样本复杂度和经典后处理复杂度，估计M个候选系数达到逐元素精度$\epsilon$。切比雪夫-洛巴托方法的后均值成本线性于M。

Conclusion: 局部响应反演可作为从实验可获取的短时数据中学习、校准和诊断复杂量子系统的可扩展范式，为开放量子系统的生成元学习提供了高效理论框架。

Abstract: Learning the generator of an open many-body system is more challenging than Hamiltonian learning: local responses, which can directly reveal coherent interaction terms in closed-system dynamics, may also contain dissipative contributions in open-system dynamics. In this paper, we address this challenge by developing an efficient Lindbladian learning framework for a known local candidate generator dictionary with bounded dissipative support and either bounded dual-interaction-graph degree or bounded unweighted local strength. The framework resolves the coherent-dissipative ambiguity by treating local Pauli responses as a linear system over both types of generator terms. Inverting this response system separates their contributions and makes the individual Lindbladian coefficients accessible from local response data in a fixed short-time window. Within this framework, we develop two efficient learning algorithms: Chebyshev--Lobatto response interpolation, which uses logarithmically many short evolution times and has a post-mean cost linear in $M$, with the stated dependence on $ε$, and Single-time projected response contraction, which uses a single fixed evolution time and globally inverts a truncated response function. Both procedures estimate $M$ candidate coefficients to entrywise accuracy $ε$ using $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(M/ε^2)$ sample and classical post-processing complexity. Our theoretical results establish local response inversion as a scalable paradigm for learning, calibrating, and diagnosing complex quantum systems from experimentally accessible short-time data.

</details>


### [145] [On some potentials based on exponential functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25805)
*Francisco M. Fernández*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单系统的归一化方法，证明了一些基于指数函数的看似不同的势函数实际上是等价的。该方法仅要求势函数存在最小值且在径向变量趋于无穷时是有限的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了揭示基于指数函数的不同势函数之间的内在等价性，从而简化对这类势函数的分析和应用。

Method: 提出了一种简单且系统的归一化方法，该方法仅要求势函数满足两个条件：存在一个最小值，且当径向变量趋于无穷时势函数是有限的。

Result: 成功证明了某些看似不同的指数型势函数实际上是等价的。

Conclusion: 所提出的归一化方法能够揭示指数型势函数之间的等价关系，有助于简化相关物理问题的分析。

Abstract: By means of a simple and systematic normalization method we show that some apparently different potentials based on exponential functions are equivalent. Present normalization method only requires that the potential exhibits a minimum and is finite when the radial variable tends to infinity.

</details>


### [146] [Optimal Quantum Eigenvalue Transformation via Linear Combinations of Hermitian Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25812)
*Yanqiao Wang,Yixuan Liang,Hongjia Chen,Jin-Peng Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种线性组合埃尔米特矩阵（LCHM）公式，用于实现一般非正规矩阵的特征值变换，并基于此发展了量子特征值变换（QET）算法，在电路深度和后选择重复次数上达到最优，统一了多种量子线性代数问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子特征值变换方法主要针对正规矩阵（如酉矩阵或埃尔米特矩阵），而一般非正规矩阵的特征值变换缺乏通用且高效的量子算法。本文旨在填补这一空白，为任意非正规矩阵提供统一的量子特征值变换框架。

Method: 1. 提出vanilla LCHM公式：将A分解为L+iH（L, H为埃尔米特矩阵），通过核积分将g(A)表示为g(i(H+kL))的线性组合。2. 提出Weyl LCHM公式：利用角度埃尔米特矩阵X_θ=cosθ L+sinθ H，将g(A)表示为g(e^{iθ}(X_θ±i(I-X_θ^2)^{1/2}))的积分。3. 对矩阵幂A^m，利用傅里叶投影得到精确离散公式，无需截断和角向求积误差。4. 基于LCHM公式设计量子特征值变换算法。

Result: 1. 对于度-d多项式p_d(A)，QET算法达到最优Θ(d)电路深度和最优O(||p_d||_∞/||p_d(A)|ψ⟩||)后选择重复次数。2. 对于多种量子线性代数问题（驱动ODE、迭代方法、预解式、log(I+A)、(λI+A)^ν、Sign和ReLU变换、非圆形域上的Faber逼近），实现近最优的Õ(d log(d/ε)) Clifford+T门复杂度。

Conclusion: LCHM公式为非正规矩阵的量子特征值变换提供了通用框架，其离散公式精确无近似误差。基于LCHM的QET算法统一了多种量子线性代数问题，并在电路深度和后选择效率上达到理论最优，具有重要的理论意义和实际应用价值。

Abstract: We discover two complementary linear-combination-of-Hermitian-matrices (LCHM) formulations to achieve a general non-normal matrix eigenvalue transformation $g(A)$. Firstly, for $A=L+\mathrm{i} H$ with Hermitian $L$ and $H$, the vanilla LCHM formula represents $g(A)$ as a kernel integral of $g(\mathrm{i}(H+kL))$, and it contains linear-combination-of-Hamiltonian-simulation (LCHS) [An, Liu, Lin, Phys. Rev. Lett. 2023] as the special case for matrix exponentials. Secondly, for the angular Hermitian $X_θ= \cosθL+\sinθH$, the Weyl LCHM formula expresses $g(A)$ via integrating $g(\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}θ} (X_θ\pm\mathrm{i}(I-X_θ^2)^{1/2}))$. For the matrix power $g(A)=A^m$, the Fourier projection of Weyl LCHM gives \[ A^m=\frac{2}π\int_0^π\text{e}^{\text{i} mθ}T_m(X_θ) \text{d}θ= \frac{2}{N}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1} \text{e}^{\text{i} mθ_j}T_m(X_{θ_j}),\quadθ_j=\frac{πj}{N},\quad \text{for every } N>m \] with Chebyshev polynomial of Hermitian $T_m(X_θ)$ and $N$ samples. The discrete formula is exact, introduces no truncation and angular quadrature error, and offers $\mathcal{O}(1)$ post-selection weights.
  LCHM formulas lead to new quantum eigenvalue transformation (QET) algorithms. For a degree-$d$ polynomial $p_d(A)$ on $|ψ\rangle$, our QET algorithm can achieve optimal $Θ(d)$ circuit depth and optimal $\mathcal{O}(||p_d||_{\infty}/||p_d(A)|ψ\rangle||)$ post-selection repetitions. LCHM-based QETs unify various quantum linear algebraic problems with near-optimal $\mathcal{\widetilde O}(d\log(d/ε))$ Clifford$+T$ gates, including driven ODEs (reduced to standard LCHS), iterative methods, resolvents, $\log(I+A)$, $(λI+A)^ν$, Sign and ReLU transforms, and Faber approximation on noncircular domains.

</details>


### [147] [Bipartite Bound Information Exists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25838)
*Jef Pauwels,Nicolas Gisin,Renato Renner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了"绑定信息"的存在性——一种类似于绑定纠缠的经典密码学现象：某些关联在生成时需要消耗秘密比特，但无法从中提取任何秘密密钥。给出了一个两比特一三进制分布的显式示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gisin和Wolf在25年前提出经典密码学中是否存在类似绑定纠缠的现象，即是否存在这样的关联：生成时需要消耗秘密比特，却无法蒸馏出任何密钥。本文旨在回答这一长期悬而未决的问题。

Method: 利用两种窃听者比较方式之间的差距：一个窃听者可能在所有互信息比较中都优于另一个，却仍然无法模拟对方的数据。通过构造一个两比特一三进制的概率分布，并结合绑定纠缠qutrit态的测量分析来证明。

Result: (1) 证明了绑定信息的存在，并给出了显式构造；(2) 发现标准基下测量绑定纠缠qutrit态得到的分布并不构成绑定信息——只要生成时消耗了秘密性，就一定能提取密钥；(3) 但测量这些态的纯化版本（即使是可分离态）则能产生绑定信息。

Conclusion: 量子绑定纠缠与经典绑定信息之间的类比应建立在资源层面而非具体状态及其测量结果层面。这表明不可逆性在量子信息和经典信息论中具有深层联系，但对应关系比预期更为微妙。

Abstract: Bound entanglement is an extreme irreversibility of quantum theory: certain states cost entanglement to create, yet no singlet can be distilled from them. Twenty-five years ago, Gisin and Wolf asked whether classical cryptography admits the same phenomenon. Are there correlations, shared by two parties and an eavesdropper, that cost secret bits to create although none can be extracted? We show that such bound information exists and give an explicit example, a distribution of two bits and a trit. The proof exploits a gap between two ways of comparing eavesdroppers: one can be better informed than another in every mutual-information comparison and nevertheless unable to simulate the other's data. We further prove that the distributions which motivated the conjecture, standard-basis measurements of bound-entangled qutrit states, are not themselves examples: a secret key is extractable from them whenever their creation costs any secrecy. Other measurements of their purifications, in contrast, do yield bound information, even for the separable states among them. The analogy is thus one of resources, not of individual states and their measurement outcomes.

</details>


### [148] [OmniQEC: discovering practical quantum error-correcting codes by an AI scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25865)
*Ge Yan,Shanchuan Li,Pengyue Ma,Qixin Zhang,Pingchuan Ma,Jianping Wang,Min-Hsiu Hsieh,Yuxuan Du*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: OmniQEC是一个利用大语言模型(LLM)作为编排器，通过快慢协同搜索流程自动发现适用于现代量子处理器的量子纠错码(QEC codes)的AI科学家系统。该系统在多个qLDPC码族和物理量子比特预算下发现的码在逻辑错误抑制方面优于BB码，为LLM辅助的QEC码-电路-解码器协同设计开辟了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错(QEC)对于可扩展容错量子计算不可或缺，但发现有效的QEC码极具挑战性，因为逻辑性能取决于码结构、硬件、综合征提取和解码器之间的复杂相互作用，这些因素往往提出相互竞争的要求。现有方法难以高效地发现适合现代量子处理器部署的QEC码。

Method: OmniQEC将QEC设计表述为一个迭代发现过程：由先进大语言模型(LLM)实现的编排器协调码生成、码级筛选、综合征提取合成和基于解码器的电路评估。核心机制结合了自演化推理与慢-快协同工作流：快循环使用廉价的码级代理探索候选码，慢循环执行基于物理的电路级评估并将证据反馈回搜索过程。

Result: 在四个qLDPC码族、三个LLM后端和每个后端14个总物理量子比特预算下进行评测，发现的码在逻辑错误抑制方面随物理量子比特预算增加而持续改善，并在98和240物理量子比特的完整实现预算下分别优于[72,12,6]和[144,12,12]的BB码。

Conclusion: 所发现的码具有硬件友好性，对实际QEC实现具有独立价值。该工作为基于物理信息驱动的码-电路-解码器协同设计的LLM辅助QEC发现奠定了基础。

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) is indispensable for scalable fault-tolerant quantum computing. However, discovering QEC codes that remain effective is challenging, as logical performance depends on the interplay between code structure, hardware, syndrome extraction, and decoding, which often impose competing requirements. Here we introduce OmniQEC, an efficient AI scientist for discovering QEC codes suited to deployment on modern quantum processors. OmniQEC formulates QEC design as an iterative discovery process in which an orchestrator, implemented by advanced large language models (LLMs), coordinates code generation, code-level screening, syndrome-extraction synthesis, and decoder-based circuit evaluation. At its core, OmniQEC combines a self-evolving reasoning mechanism with a slow--fast synergistic workflow: a fast loop explores candidates using inexpensive code-level proxies, whereas a slow loop performs physically grounded circuit-level evaluation and feeds the resulting evidence back into the search. We evaluate OmniQEC across four qLDPC construction families, three LLM backends, and $14$ total-physical-qubit budgets per backend. The discovered codes show steadily improving logical-error suppression with increasing physical-qubit budgets and outperform the BB codes with $[\![72,12,6]\!]$ and $[\![144,12,12]\!]$ under complete-implementation budgets of 98 and 240 physical qubits, respectively. The discovered codes are hardware-friendly and may be of independent interest for practical QEC implementation. These findings pave the way towards LLM-assisted QEC discovery grounded in physically informed code--circuit--decoder co-design.

</details>


### [149] [Consistent Quantum States over Spacetime without a Common Quantum Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25899)
*Jianqi Sheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明存在多个记录条件量子描述虽共享相同无条件量子态，但无法由单一确定过程实现，并建立了可操作性的判据与可见性阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定多个记录条件量子描述是否共享单一潜在过程，是量子态时空（QSOST）与关系性量子描述的核心一致性问题。然而因果无关干涉测量仅能访问低维QSOST投影，遗留下过程自由度未解。

Method: 结合分支分辨QSOST干涉测量、正过程升维技术与半定对偶性，证明每个正权重QSOST分支存在唯一最小正升维，将设定级与公共过程可实现性归结为确定过程支配。构造最小二分量子比特示例（A≺B因果序，两个记录设定和两个结果）。

Result: 每个设定单独可实现，两者共享相同完整无条件QSOST，但不存在单一确定过程联合实现它们；分离的可见度阈值为η=1/√2，存在包含八个干涉正交分量的稀疏见证，且对满秩过程成立。

Conclusion: 建立了QSOST一致性与全局过程一致性之间的操作性边界，为检验多个量子视角能否共享单一标准量子过程提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Determining whether multiple record-conditioned quantum descriptions admit a single underlying process is a central consistency problem for quantum states over spacetime (QSOSTs) and relational quantum descriptions. Yet causally agnostic interferometry accesses only a lower-dimensional QSOST projection, which inevitably leaves some process degrees of freedom unresolved. We combine branch-resolved QSOST interferometry with positive-process lifting and semidefinite convex duality to characterize this gluing problem. We prove that every positive-weight QSOST branch has a unique least positive lift and reduce both settingwise and common-process realizability to deterministic-process domination. Using this criterion, we construct a minimal bipartite-qubit example with two record settings and two outcomes in the definite causal order $A\prec B$: each setting is separately realizable, and both share the same complete unconditional QSOST, yet no single deterministic process realizes them jointly. The separation has the exact visibility threshold $η=1/\sqrt{2}$ , admits a sparse witness involving eight interferometric quadratures, and persists for full-rank processes. These results establish an operational boundary between QSOST consistency and global process consistency, and provide a practical framework for testing whether multiple quantum perspectives can share one standard-quantum process.

</details>


### [150] [Effect-valued measurement models and contextuality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25900)
*Samson Abramsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将概率分布推广到凸效应代数上的分布，建立了一个统一的非语境性理论框架，涵盖了概率、确定性和量子测量结构，并揭示了内部语境性与可观察语境性之间的关系，通过锥规划表征非语境性，引入效应值语境分数，并分析了测量模型的锐可实现性与均匀扩张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Sheaf理论语境性处理局限于概率分布，无法统一描述概率、确定性和量子测量结构。作者希望建立一个更一般的框架，使用凸效应代数作为值域，将各种测量模型纳入统一形式体系，并澄清内部语境性与可观察语境性之间的关系。

Method: 将Abramsky-Brandenburger的层论语境性框架中的概率分布替换为凸效应代数A上的分布；利用态σ:A→[0,1]将效应值模型转化为普通经验模型；使用效应模的有序向量空间表示，将非语境性刻画为关联锥规划的可解性；通过锥对偶引入Bell见证；分析锐可实现性和均匀扩张。

Result: 建立了A值测量模型统一框架；区分了内部语境性与可观察语境性；证明了非语境性等价于锥规划可行性；引入了效应值语境分数；推导了Bell见证作为Bell不等式的推广；阐明了锐可实现性与均匀扩张的条件；展示了资源索引效应结构诱导分级单子，推广了量子单子。

Conclusion: 该工作为语境性研究提供了一个高度统一的数学框架，将概率、确定性和量子语境性纳入同一体系，并通过凸分析和层论工具建立了新的理论结果，为量子基础研究和资源理论提供了新的视角和工具。

Abstract: We generalize the Abramsky--Brandenburger sheaf-theoretic treatment of contextuality by replacing probability distributions with distributions valued in a convex effect algebra \(A\). This yields a notion of \(A\)-valued measurement model encompassing probabilistic, deterministic, and quantum measurement structures within a single framework.
  States \(σ:A\to[0,1]\) induce ordinary empirical models, allowing observable behaviour to be viewed as arising from effect-valued measurement data. This leads to a distinction between internal contextuality of \(A\)-models and observable contextuality after state evaluation. We analyze the relationship between these notions and identify coherence conditions under which observable classical explanations assemble into internal ones.
  Using the ordered-vector-space representation of effect modules, we show that non-contextuality is characterized by feasibility of an associated cone program, generalizing the linear-programming formulation of contextuality in the probabilistic case. We also introduce an effect-valued contextual fraction and study its relation to observable contextuality witnesses. We show how cone duality leads to a notion of Bell witnesses for contextuality as a direct generalization of Bell inequalities.
  Finally, we analyze sharp realizability and uniform dilation for measurement models, clarifying the relationship between general POVMs and projective measurements, and show how resource-indexed effect structures induce graded monads generalizing the quantum monad.

</details>


### [151] [Sampling hard circuits with verifiably high fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25941)
*Simon Martiel,Jay-U Chung,Alireza Seif,Soumik Ghosh,Ian Hincks,Abhinav Deshpande,Bill Fefferman,Jay M. Gambetta,Ali Javadi-Abhari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合可证明计算复杂性与量子纠错的采样型量子计算方案，通过时空码编码70量子比特、70深度的Clifford电路（掺杂468个T门），实现了10倍的错误抑制和0.284的保真度下限（95%置信度），同时保留了基于码综合征的保真度认证能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采样型量子计算方案虽然具有复杂性理论的硬度保证，但难以同时满足可扩展量子计算所需的两个关键能力：抑制硬件错误和对量子计算本身进行验证。作者旨在解决这一矛盾。

Method: 引入结构化电路，这些电路既保留了可证明的硬度保证，又允许在量子码中编码。通过码综合征测量来认证实验保真度，并利用时空码对70量子比特、深度70的Clifford电路（掺杂468个T门）进行编码，使用97个物理量子比特实现。

Result: 经过综合征后选择，门错误率被有效抑制了10倍；得到的量子态在95%置信度下具有0.284的保真度下限。

Conclusion: 该构造提供了一种系统方法，将稳定子态提升为魔法态，同时保持错误检测保真度证书，为兼具计算复杂性和容错性的量子计算方案开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Sampling-based proposals are prominent candidates for demonstrating quantum computations beyond the reach of classical supercomputers. However, it has been difficult to combine their complexity-theoretic hardness with two capabilities needed for scalable quantum computing more generally: suppressing hardware errors, and verifying the quantum computation itself. Here we address both issues by introducing structured circuits, which, in addition to provable hardness guarantees, admit an encoding in a quantum code. This allows us to simultaneously reach high fidelities at high circuit depths, and to certify an experimental fidelity via the circuit structure and measurement of code syndromes. The resulting certificate is device dependent, but requires substantially weaker noise assumptions than existing fidelity proxy benchmarks. We demonstrate our proposal with a $70$-qubit, depth-$70$ Clifford circuit doped with $468$ $T$ gates. We use a total of $97$ physical qubits to encode this computation in spacetime codes, effectively suppressing gate error rates by $10\times$ after syndrome post-selection, and yielding a state with a fidelity lower bound of $0.284$ with $95\%$ confidence. Our construction is a systematic method for promoting a stabilizer state to a magic state while keeping an error-detected fidelity certificate.

</details>


### [152] [Equi-Entropic Maps for Four-Partite Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25954)
*Wojciech Bruzda,Zahra Raissi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究四体高维系统中一种弱化的均匀纠缠形式，通过引入线性映射Ξ来强制三个平衡二分划具有相等的线性熵（不要求最大），并分析了该映射的代数结构、不动点、渐近行为及其与双酉矩阵和正交拉丁方的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对最大纠缠态约束过强，作者希望研究一种更弱的均匀纠缠形式，即仅要求三个平衡二分划熵相等而不必达到最大值，以探索更广泛的纠缠结构。

Method: 引入线性映射Ξ，通过重排和部分转置强制熵相等；该映射可看作迭代平均过程的渐近极限，并具有置换群论描述。对Haar随机酉输入，采用前阶矩分析并结合数值模拟。

Result: 对于Haar随机酉输入，映射的输出具有高度纠缠特性，其公共熵随局域维数增长趋近最大值。同时刻画了映射的代数结构、不动点及渐近行为，并揭示了与双酉矩阵和正交拉丁方的关联。

Conclusion: 这种弱化的均匀纠缠态在四体高维系统中存在且可通过映射Ξ有效构造，其与双酉矩阵和正交拉丁方的联系为纠缠结构的研究提供了新的代数视角。

Abstract: Absolutely maximally entangled states represent a highly constrained form of multipartite entanglement and play an important role in quantum information theory. We investigate a weaker form of uniformity of entanglement for four-party systems of local dimension $d>2$ that requires the three balanced bipartitions to have equal but not necessarily maximal linear entropy. We introduce a linear map $Ξ$ that enforces exact equality of entropies under reshuffling and partial transposition. The transformation arises as the asymptotic limit of an iterative averaging procedure and admits a group-theoretic description in terms of permutations of tensor indices. For Haar-random unitary inputs, a leading-moment analysis supported by numerical simulations predicts highly entangled outputs whose common entropy approaches the maximal value as the local dimension grows. We characterize the algebraic structure, fixed points, and asymptotic behavior of this map and its relation to two-unitary matrices and orthogonal Latin squares.

</details>


### [153] [Observable Estimation in the Absence of Classical Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25998)
*Samantha V. Barron,Bradley Mitchell,Vinay Tripathi,Francesco Grieco,Ilan Rosen,Francesca Pietracaprina,Davide Materia,Alireza Seif,Darvin Wanisch,Ramón L. Panadés-Barrueta,Ewout van den Berg,Jay-U Chung,Andrew Eddins,Sam Ferracin,Guillermo García-Pérez,John Goold,Luke C. G. Govia,Holger Haas,Ian Hincks,Jesse C. Hoke,Zoë Holmes,Su-un Lee,Youngseok Kim,Swarnadeep Majumder,Sabrina Maniscalco,Simone Montangero,Daniel Puzzuoli,Tomaž Prosen,James Raftery,Ricardo Rivera Cardoso,Max Rossmannek,Manuel Rudolph,Brendan Saxberg,Liran Shirizly,Karthik Siva,Joshua Skanes-Norman,Ilaria Siloi,Kevin C Smith,Boris Sokolov,Maika Takita,Yanting Teng,Mao Tian Tan,Joseph Tindall,Zoltán Zimborás,Matteo A. C. Rossi,Minh C. Tran,Sergei N. Filippov,Abhinav Kandala*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个在缺乏经典基准情况下独立验证量子计算结果可信性的框架，通过量子启发式实验设计和噪声模型表征，为科学发现中的可信量子计算提供了路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子模拟正进入经典近似方法难以处理的参数区间，当可靠的经典基准不可用时，如何信任量子计算结果成为一个根本性挑战。本文旨在解决这一验证困境。

Method: 1. 提出用于独立验证量子估计的框架；2. 引入算子Loschmidt回声这一新概念；3. 系统设计一系列量子启发式实验，测试底层假设并提供对量子计算机可观测估计的置信度；4. 通过表征和操控设备噪声将问题转化为验证噪声模型，从而为量子估计设置精度界限。

Result: 在令多种领先经典模拟方法失效的半扰乱动力学物理模型中，框架表明量子估计在缺乏即时可访问的ground-truth解的情况下，提供了最可信的结果。

Conclusion: 该框架为超越经典验证的可信量子计算用于科学发现建立了可行路线。

Abstract: The predictive success of quantum mechanics underpins many areas of modern science, even as the exact simulation of large, interacting quantum systems remains beyond the reach of classical computation. This success has been enabled by the remarkable advancement of scalable numerical approximation methods, which often demonstrate practical accuracy despite the absence of formal guarantees. As quantum simulation pushes into regimes where these approximations struggle, a fundamental challenge arises: How can quantum outcomes be trusted when reliable classical benchmarks are unavailable? Here, we establish a framework for the independent validation of quantum estimates in this setting and present evidence that they provide the most credible result among several considered methods, in the absence of an immediately accessible ground-truth solution. We apply our framework to the semi-scrambling dynamics of a physical model that strains several leading classical simulation methods yet remains experimentally accessible, in part through our introduction of the \textit{operator Loschmidt echo}. We systematically design a series of experiments using quantum heuristics that, taken together, test the underlying assumptions and provide strong confidence in the observable estimates obtained from the quantum computer. We then show how this framework can be extended to place accuracy bounds on quantum estimates via careful characterization and manipulation of the device noise, transforming the problem of validating the observable estimation to validating the noise model. These results establish a route towards trusted quantum computation for scientific discovery, independent of classical verification.

</details>

# math.QA [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Completing the Arakawa--Moreau Conjecture on Maximal Ideals of Affine Vertex Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25249)
*Sihai Jin*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文完整证明了Arakawa-Moreau猜想1，该猜想涉及D型和E型负水平通用仿射顶点代数中由显式奇异向量生成的理想的极大性。通过最小Drinfeld-Sokolov约化、Ramond-Zhu代数论证、Casimir间隙论证和Li谱流等方法，证明了所有剩余情况（D_ℓ的-1级（ℓ≥5）以及D_4、E_6、E_7、E_8的n>0负水平情况），从而确认所有候选商都是对应的简单仿射顶点代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arakawa和Moreau构造了D型和E型负水平通用仿射顶点代数中的显式奇异向量，并猜想由这些向量生成的理想是极大的。此前工作已证明部分情况，但仍有剩余情况未解决，本文旨在完成该猜想的完整证明。

Method: 采用最小Drinfeld-Sokolov约化确定奇异向量像，结合Ramond-Zhu代数论证、Casimir间隙论证和Li谱流建立约化商的单纯性；再利用约化函子的正合性和非消没定理将单纯性提升到仿射商；还提出了基于最小约化的广义极大性原理，给出了D_ℓ已知-2级结果的替代约化理论证明，以及D_{2r}的V^{2-2r}(D_{2r}) collapsing族的极大理想定理的秩约化证明。

Result: 完整证明了Arakawa-Moreau猜想1的所有剩余情况：D_ℓ（ℓ≥5）的-1级情况，以及D_4、E_6、E_7、E_8的n>0负水平情况。结合已有结果，猜想1完全得证。

Conclusion: Arakawa-Moreau猜想1中每个候选商都是对应的简单仿射顶点代数，该猜想被完全解决。

Abstract: Arakawa and Moreau constructed explicit singular vectors in a family of negative-level universal affine vertex algebras of types $D$ and $E$ and conjectured that the ideals generated by these vectors are maximal. Previous work established the $n=0$ cases for $D_4$, $E_6$, $E_7$, and $E_8$, as well as the level $-2$ case for $D_\ell$ with $\ell\geq 5$. We prove all the remaining cases: the level $-1$ case for $D_\ell$ with $\ell\geq 5$, and the negative-level cases with $n>0$ for $D_4$, $E_6$, $E_7$, and $E_8$. Together with the previously known results, this completes Arakawa--Moreau Conjecture 1.
  The proof determines the images of the prescribed singular vectors under minimal Drinfeld--Sokolov reduction and establishes simplicity of the reduced quotients by combining a Ramond--Zhu algebra argument, a Casimir-gap argument, and Li's spectral flow. Exactness and a nonvanishing theorem for the reduction functor then lift simplicity to the corresponding affine quotients. We also formulate a general maximality principle based on minimal reduction, give an alternative reduction-theoretic proof of the known level $-2$ result for $D_\ell$, and obtain a rank-reduction proof of the maximal-ideal theorem for the collapsing family $V^{2-2r}(D_{2r})$. Consequently, every candidate quotient appearing in Arakawa--Moreau Conjecture 1 is the corresponding simple affine vertex algebra.

</details>


### [155] [Calabi-Yau Deformation Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25438)
*Jakob Ulmer*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文记录了卡拉比-丘版本的孔采维奇形式性态射，从形变量子化的角度出发。特别地，发现任何单模全纯泊松卡拉比-丘流形在其解析函数代数上具有规范闭形变量子化。更广泛的动机是通过Sen-Zwiebach的弦顶点在卡拉比-丘范畴的背景下解释孔采维奇的形变量子化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更广泛的动机是在卡拉比-丘范畴的设定下，通过Sen-Zwiebach的弦顶点来解释Kontsevich的形变量子化。

Method: 记录了卡拉比-丘版本的Kontsevich形式性态射，从形变量子化出发。具体化为单模全纯泊松卡拉比-丘流形的闭形变量子化。

Result: 任何单模全纯泊松卡拉比-丘流形在其解析函数代数上都有一个规范的闭形变量子化。

Conclusion: 建立了卡拉比-丘版本的形式性态射，为泊松卡拉比-丘流形提供了规范的闭形变量子化，并指出更广泛的动机是通过弦顶点在卡拉比-丘范畴中解释形变量子化。

Abstract: We record the Calabi-Yau version of Kontsevich's formality morphism from deformation quantization. As a special case we find that any unimodular holomorphic Poisson Calabi-Yau has a canonical closed deformation quantization of its resolved algebra of functions. A broader motivation is to find an explanation of Kontsevich's deformation quantization in the setting of Calabi-Yau categories via Sen-Zwiebach's string vertices.

</details>


### [156] [Construction of PBW and canonical bases in the folding extension algebras for symmetrizable types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25495)
*Yumeng Wu*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文通过Lusztig的perverse sheaf构造，研究了带可容许自同构a的箭图表示空间上的扩张代数，从而折叠了KLR代数。在可对称化框架下，构造了斜群代数R(ν)⟨a⟩，证明了Extᵇ(⋅,L)保持带符号基和晶体结构，并在有限型中定义了标准与对偶标准模，证明了投射模典范基到对偶标准模PBW基的转移矩阵是上三角且对角元为1，实现了这些基及其关系的几何范畴化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Varagnolo和Vasserot的方法推广到Lusztig的可对称化设定中，为带可容许自同构的箭图表示空间上的KLR代数（折叠KLR代数）提供几何范畴化理论基础。

Method: 构造斜群代数R(ν)⟨a⟩（其中a由可容许自同构a诱导），证明Extᵇ(⋅,L)保持带符号基和晶体结构；在有限型中定义标准模和对偶标准模，分析其对偶性行为，研究Grothendieck群结构，并证明转移矩阵的上三角性及对角元为1。

Result: 证明了从投射模（典范基）到对偶标准模（PBW基）的转移矩阵是上三角的且对角元为1，从而在可对称化框架下几何范畴化了这些基及其相互关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为折叠KLR代数在可对称化设定中提供了几何范畴化，建立了典范基与PBW基之间的清晰联系，推广了Varagnolo-Vasserot的方法。

Abstract: We study extension algebras arising from Lusztig's construction of perverse sheaves on quiver representation spaces equipped with an admissible automorphism $a$, leading to folding Khovanov--Lauda--Rouquier (KLR) algebras. Extending the method of M. Varagnolo and E. Vasserot to Lusztig's symmetrizable setting, we construct the skew group algebra $R(ν)\langle\mathbf{a}\rangle$, where $\mathbf{a}$ is induced by the admissible automorphism $a$, and show that $\Ext^{\bullet}(\ ,L)$ preserves the signed basis and the crystal structure. In this framework, for finite types, we define standard and dual standard modules, analyze their behavior under dualities, and study the resulting Grothendieck groups. We prove that the transition matrix from the classes of indecomposable projective modules, which give the canonical basis, to the dual standard modules, which give the PBW basis, is upper triangular with diagonal entries equal to one. This provides a geometric categorification of these bases and of their relation in the symmetrizable setting.

</details>


### [157] [Graph integrals, Feynman periods, and single-valued multiple zeta values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25595)
*Jean-Luc Portner*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文证明，图的原始规范积分与形变量子化中的位置空间积分一致，从而推出规范积分求值为单值多重zeta值，并且每个单值多重zeta值都可表示为无质量传播子费曼周期的有理线性组合。此外，在交换图复形中，两个相关的上循环是一致的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Borel类生成的线性群稳定上同调可由不变微分形式表示。沿热带Torelli映射拉回这些形式会得到与图相关的规范收敛积分，这些积分与GL_n上同调及图复形密切相关。一个自然的问题是这些图积分究竟对应什么数值。

Method: 通过建立原始规范积分与形变量子化中位置空间积分族之间的等同关系，利用代数结构和解析性质进行对应。

Result: 1) 原始规范积分等于形变量子化中的位置空间积分；2) 规范积分求值为单值多重zeta值；3) 每个单值多重zeta值都是无质量传播子费曼周期的有理线性组合；4) 交换图复形中两个相关上循环一致。

Conclusion: 建立了图积分、形变量子化、单值多重zeta值与费曼周期之间的深刻联系，揭示了Borel类生成稳定上同调与量子场论中费曼积分之间的桥梁。

Abstract: The Borel classes generating the stable cohomology of the general linear group can be represented by invariant differential forms. It is known that pulling these forms back along a tropical Torelli map yields canonical convergent integrals associated to graphs, which are closely connected to the cohomology of $\mathrm{GL}_n$ and of graph complexes. A natural question is what numbers these graph integrals are. We answer this for primitive canonical integrals by showing that they coincide with a family of complex position-space integrals arising in deformation quantisation. As a consequence, canonical integrals of graphs evaluate to single-valued multiple zeta values. We further deduce that every single-valued multiple zeta value occurs as a rational linear combination of Feynman periods of graphs with massless propagators. Finally, in the commutative graph complex, our result implies that the two associated cocycles agree.

</details>
